526. **(2026-11-15) Autoplay in the Danmakusu column.** The user: "Move Autoplay to Danmakusu column". The column patch (`ModsColumnInSongSelect`) takes the Automation column's Danmakusu mods - its own Autoplay - out of it and shows them after the boss selections in the Danmakusu column (osu! gives every column its mods in order, ours first; when Automation is given its mods, ours is given them again with the Autoplay). The mod stays an Automation mod to osu! (Ctrl+Enter, multiplayer and the rest as before). If osu! will not have it in another column, it stays in Automation and the log says so.
527. **(2026-11-15) The freeze when a map starts: what the log now tells.** The user: "When game starts - game freezes and it starts before screen unfreezes", "it happens when there is no boss menu select". The last log: the play's screen came at 05:16:28 and the field's first frame only at 05:16:31, already at 1.3 s of the map (the music's clock went on while the game's thread stood); with the boss menu the map waits behind it, so the same wait is not seen. The field is built while osu! loads the player (in the background), so the three seconds are after it, and nothing of ours that runs then is heavy (the boss stars are drawn in the background, the calculator skips the rating object by object). New lines in the log to find it: `the field was made in N ms (on the ... thread)`, `the playfield is ready, at T ms of the map`, `the playfield's first frame, N ms after it was ready, at T ms of the map`, and `the game stood N ms before the frame at T ms of the map` for any wait over half a second between two frames.
528. **(2026-11-15) Matenshi: her wings are bones and feathers, not slices.** The user: "you sliced her wings wrongly. You need to segment her feathers and bones instead". The six slices of the angle round the shoulder (496) are gone. New `UI/Stages/WingRig.cs`: the wing is a bird's wing read off the picture - three bones along the leading edge (shoulder, elbow, wrist, the hand's tip), each with the covering feathers on it (the humerus carries the mass at the shoulder), and 18 long feathers each on its own: 7 primaries on the hand, 6 secondaries on the forearm, 5 tertials at the elbow (each a root on its bone and a tip). Every pixel belongs to the bone or feather it is nearest to (a bone as far as its covering reaches, 55/26/18 px; a feather 10 px round its shaft), and neighbours overlap more towards the tips (`WingRig.OverlapAt`, 3.5 to 14 px) so that no gap opens. The bones sway as the wing does, each joint further out more and later, as a whip (`MATENSHI_WING_FLEX_DEGREES` 2 more a joint, `MATENSHI_BONE_LAG_BEATS` 0.25 later a joint); each feather is carried by its bone, follows its sway later the further out it grows (`MATENSHI_FEATHER_LAG_BEATS` 0.15, up to three times that at the hand) and flutters on its own as before. The pieces are drawn feathers first, then the bones over their roots; each piece's texture is cut to its own bounds (42 textures, loaded only when she is on the map; `StageTexture.WingRightPiece0..20`/`WingLeftPiece0..20` for `WingRightFeather0..5`/`WingLeftFeather0..5`). The wings hurt, and the feathers she fires come out, where each piece has moved. Which piece every pixel is on is worked out when her act is made (0.2 s more to load a map with her). Bench (Chirality, 0:20 + 60 s, the autoplay): 0 hits, as before. New tool modes: `wingrig <wing.png> <out.png>` (the pieces in colours) and `wingpose <wing.png> <out.png> <beats,...>` (the wing posed at those beats). Her description says so.
529. **(2026-11-15) The map waits until the game draws smoothly.** The user played once more with the new log lines (527): the field was made in 7.6 s while osu! loaded (in the background: not seen), the playfield's first frame came at once at -1025 ms of the map, and then the game stood 2.15 s while the music's clock went on: the next frame was at 1151 ms. Whatever makes those first frames wait, the map no longer runs during it: as the boss menu does, the gameplay clock is held when a map starts until 8 frames in a row each came within 100 ms of the one before (`START_QUICK_FRAMES`, `START_QUICK_FRAME_MS`; never longer than 10 s, `START_HOLD_MOST_MS`), then started. The hold (`DanmakusuPlayfield.StartHold`) is a component of osu!'s gameplay clock container, beside the HUD, so that it is updated every frame while the clock stands (the frame-stable playfield may not be). With the boss menu, the menu holds the map while it is open (`HeldByMenu`) and its Start hands the start back to the hold (`StartWhenSmooth`), which waits out the field being made again for the chosen bosses too. The log says `the map starts at T ms, held N ms until the game drew smoothly`. Not tried in osu! yet.
530. **(2026-11-15) The start freeze found: the bullet sheets were drawn on the game's thread.** The user: "again" (the hold of 529 did not help: its first frame came only after the 2.2 s, and osu!'s clock had gone on through it although osu! had not started it yet), "Fix freezing, try again and again until its resolved", "excuse of not knowing what causes not accepted". The 2.2 s were between the playfield's first frame and the bullet field's: the field's `LoadComplete` drew the bullet sheets for its palette (`BulletRenderer.SetPalette`: every bullet shape - disc, arrowhead, star, heart, petal, slice, maple, note, feather, soft star - for every colour, up to 40 with the bosses' colours, sharp and blurred soft: 1.2-1.3 s in the tools, more in the game's unoptimised build, then two 33 MB sheets for the graphics card). Now:
    - The sheets are drawn in the field's load, while osu! loads the play in the background; at its start the field only checks that the colours are the same (nothing is drawn again), and a skin changed during the play is drawn in the background (`SetPaletteLater`). The renderer no longer draws a stand-in palette of its own first (half a second for nothing). The colours' rows are drawn at once on all cores: 40 colours 1.2 s -> 0.5 s (`sectioncheck palettetime`, new).
    - The ruleset is compiled with optimisations (`<Optimize>true</Optimize>`: the build osu! runs is the Debug one): the rating 15 % faster, the same stars (Chirality 6.73).
    - The nine boss figures of a field share one texture per picture (`BossCharacter.LoadShared`; so do the HUD's heads): with 98 bosses 98 textures instead of 882, read and sent to the graphics card once.
    - The boss menu's Start makes the field for the chosen bosses in the background (`ChooseBosses`, `Rebuilding`) and swaps it in when it is loaded; made on the game's thread it stood the game up to 8 s.
    - The hold (529) stays as a safety: the map starts from where it stood at the playfield's first frame - if the clock has gone on meanwhile it is put back (`the map had gone on to T ms while the game stood: put back to ...`) - and waits for the menu's field too.

532. **(2026-11-18) osu! 2026.1117.0.** The user: "adapt to new update". osu! had updated itself to 2026.1117.0 (the ruleset was built for 2026.916.0): every map crashed as it loaded (`MissingMethodException: AudioManager.GetSampleStore(IResourceStore<byte[]>, AudioMixer)`, in the field's load of its sounds - osu!framework changed the method; the source is the same, only the compiled call was not there any more). NuGet has osu!framework 2026.1117.0 but the game's packages only up to 2026.1026.0: the csproj now takes the framework at 2026.1117.0, the game's packages at 2026.1026.0 for the libraries, and compiles against the installed lazer's own `osu.Game`, `osu.Game.Rulesets.Osu` and `osu.Game.Rulesets.Mania` (`UseInstalledLazer`, `LazerDir`): the ruleset sees exactly what osu! runs. New `..\Tools\RefCheck`: looks up every type, method and field the built dll takes from other assemblies in the installed lazer, by name and signature. The old dll: 1 missing of 3880 (that method, nothing else); the new: 0. The patches (other modes offered, the stars in song select, the counts, the mod column, the purple mods) all applied on the new osu! (its log; `sectioncheck sortstars` / `offered`), and the names looked up by reflection are all there - but one, which was never there (below). SectionCheck and LazerImport (and the watcher's copies, `SectionCheck\bin\Stars`, `LazerImport\bin\Watch`) are built the same way and run on the lazer's own dlls; the bench plays as before (Hecatia, Mima, Reimu 0 hits). SKILLS.md: "When osu! updates".
    - The library's rating: when osu! closed while it rated maps in the background, every map it was on failed (`ObjectDisposedException`) and was kept as one that cannot be rated, for good (they kept osu!'s stars). Now it stops and rates them next time. The 654 maps `danmakusu_stars.csv` had as unratable were all of that (the 19 logs osu! keeps have 4748 such failures and no other): taken out, rated again at the next start (a map that really cannot be rated is kept as such again).
    - Not changed, to ask: the colour of the map's background picture (`BackgroundColour.Of`: beams and stars of spinners and bosses in it) looks for `BeatmapSetInfo.GetPathForFile` by reflection, which osu! does not have (not in 2026.916.0 either: it is an extension method): it has never found the picture, and the combo colours have always been used.

Her fans: `AYA_FAN_BULLETS` 5 → 10, `AYA_FAN_EVERY_BEATS` 2 → 1, maple leaves (`BulletShape.Maple`), orange (read "maple shaped fans" as fans of maple leaves). `AYA_DASH_SPEED` 4 → 8; she sets off on the half beats (new `AYA_DASH_EVERY_BEATS` 0.5; it was the beat), `AYA_PREVIEW_LEAST_BEATS` 0.5 → 0.25. Stars 4 → 5.

(4, 2, 1 and 6 were tried first: 15 ms a frame with the autoplay.) Bench with the autoplay 7.96 ms a frame, 0 hits. Stars 3 → 4.

The held shots: a stretch's freeze could catch what it had just let go, born a hair before (rounding); the freezes now begin half a millisecond earlier (`BossOps` Fossil, `BornBefore`). The bench showed the held shots going out before and after, so the fix is a guess at what the user saw.

No pauses: what is left of her section after the last whole sphere (more than two beats) gets a shorter one. (`SAPPHIRE_REST_BEATS` was 0 already.)

The autoplay asked for the cracks hundreds of times a frame: the pieces are now made once (`stompPieces`, `fissurePieces`) and the tails tested in place; Zenithfall 1:00 with the autoplay, "ways" 4.54 → 0.79 ms a frame, worst frame 81 → 36, 0 hits.

560. **(2026-11-22) Okina: a beam keeps its length through the portals; Hatate's frame half as fast.** The user: "Okina still creates infinite stretching laserbeams. Rule - laserbeam total length that goes through portal should always remian the same", "Photograph boss - halve the movement speed of photo frame", then "When laserbeams go through Okina portal stretches should not happen at all, make sure cases are zero."
   - **The log** (07:42-08:09 UTC): Okina with Nazrin (Demetori - Furuki Yuanxian [Extra Stage]), Ubame (Camellia - Tornado [IOException's Gale]) and Rumia (Camellia - Exit This Earth's Atomosphere [Space Odyssey]); no piece of beam longer than 60 % of the screen on the bench, so it was not one line across it: the beams that came out of the portals were longer than they went in, in zigzags.
   - New on the bench: `PORTAL_LENGTH=1` - every run of a beam through a portal against the same slices traced without the portals (ops and all), from the points as the beam makes them (new `LaserEmitter.PointsProbe`: which beam, when every slice was fired). Only the low 16 bits of a look's `Portal` are portals (the beam's own cuts count above them). Old code (by the first, rougher count: beams cut by their own rules counted too) → new: Zenithfall 0:20 + 90 s, `long`, with Junko 186 → 0, Kokoro 4461 → 0, Kanako 108 → 0, Seija 2157 → 0; the log's maps, the whole map with its song: Ubame on Tornado 29 → 0 (81,906 runs), Nazrin on Furuki Yuanxian 49 → 0 (66,115), Rumia on Exit This Earth's Atomosphere 0 (98,826); Okina with every one of the 149 others (Zenithfall 0:20 + 30 s): 3,440,715 runs through a portal, 0 stretched (before the last fix, `beamMoves`, only Enoko had any: 262).
   - What was wrong, and what is done (`PortalAct`, `BulletField.Ops`, `LaserEmitter`):
     - A beam takes the portals as they were when it first went in (104). That moment was the earliest among the slices looked at, and a slice looked at later that had reached the wall earlier moved it, and every slice after it with it; the slices in between the beam's own points are looked at anew every frame, so every frame some of them came out somewhere else (80 px apart along the wall): the zigzags. Now the moment is the one the beam was first seen to go in at, and it never moves: every slice uses it, also one that reached the wall before.
     - A slice that ops steer started them again from where it came out, its own way, at the beam's own speed (what had slowed it or turned it before was lost), so the part that came out was another beam. Now a slice that has gone through is where the beam without the portals has it, moved and turned as the portal moves and turns what goes in (`throughPortal`, `portedSlice`; the mark keeps where it went in and came out): what comes out is the beam as it would have been, as long as it would have been, whatever the ops do. A straight slice already was so; it now keeps the speed it flew in at.
     - On two walls at a right angle (a portal sliding round a corner) what went through came out mirrored (along the wall one way, turned the other): now turned as a whole (`alongSign`), for bullets too.
     - A portal bent round a corner took in through two walls and let out through one: two ways turned, one number. The portal number has the wall it went in through too (`1 + Id * 16 + entryWall * 4 + exitWall`).
     - Where a beam's look changes (an op that touches some slices and not others: Seija's, Kokoro's) the corner the beam puts in took its neighbours' look; a corner that had not been through the portals they had was drawn joined to them from wherever it was, a line across the screen. Now the beam is cut there instead, each side at its own last slice (`insertKinks`).
     - The places along the walls did not move every slice evenly (with Enoko the part that came out was a fifth longer: its slices came out as far apart across the beam, but further apart along the wall). Now the first slice of a beam through two walls sets how the portal moves and turns that beam (`beamMoves`: where it went in, where it came out, the turn), and every later slice of it through the same walls comes out moved and turned just so - one piece by construction.
     - The portals took a beam's time through Gengetsu's clock a second time (new `TraceSliceClocked`).
   - Frame times (Zenithfall 0:20 + 60 s, `long`, ms a frame, old → new): Okina 2.96 → 3.05, with Kokoro 18.1 → 18.7, Seija 13.5 → 13.4, Junko 5.5 → 5.9, Eiki 29.7 → 30.6 (the same within the noise). Kokoro + Okina is heavy either way (worst frames of 400-550 ms, most of it "zones" and "lasers") - not changed here.
   - **Hatate.** `HATATE_FLY_BEATS` 2 → 4 (the frame flies as far, twice as long; the picture is taken when it gets there); description.
   - Not tried in the game.
561. **(2026-11-22) Yoshika's standing rings become a train; Remilia's blades hurt; Aya's feathers rimmed; Star Sapphire denser; Nina keeps what leaves.** The user (with a screenshot of Hisami + Yoshika on Justice Monster, the screen covered in rings of talismans): "Aya - white outlines on black feathers / Remilia - multi-edge knives body hit like sliders / Saphire - Sphere frames are twice more populated, stars are replaced by marisa stars / Nina Watari - When laserbeam gets active - shoot out all left bullets recorded in past 8 beats from playfield aimed towards laserbeam / Also, what is this, why circles stopped...", then "for some reason multi-edge knives blades are harmless" and "nina, warn about bullets that about to come out of edge, golden flash".

Since her curve (559) every returning talisman left copies standing all along its curve - curl, both loops and the straight - for eight beats each, so every loop stood on the screen as a ring of talismans. Read "circles stopped" so (Sakuya's time stop, the one other thing that holds things still there, does not touch circles and sliders).
   - **Yoshika.** The copies no longer stand: a train of them follows the talisman end to end along the very same legs (new `StageBullet.LegsDelay`: one leg list shared, each copy a talisman's length later), as the body of Aunn's beam follows its head, and the talisman flies on straight out of the screen after its curve with the train behind it (it ended at the end of its curve). The train is as long as the talisman goes in `YOSHIKA_TRAIN_BEATS` = `YOSHIKA_HEAD_BEATS` 1 (as many copies as she left in a beat before the curve; Aunn's 16 circle radii cost 32 ms a frame with the autoplay, 361 frames with nowhere safe). `legPoint` finds its leg by a search now (her curves have hundreds). Zenithfall 1:00 + 25 s, autoplay: 7.96 ms a frame this morning (the standing copies) → 3.1, with Hisami 3.4, 0 hits. Stars 4 → 3.
   - **Remilia.** Her knives of two, three and four blades (`REMILIA_DOUBLE_EDGED_AT` ...) hurt only in a small circle round each blade's middle: a blade away from the middle went through the player unhurt (standing still, Zenithfall 0:55 + 24 s: no knife hit further than 20 px from its middle). Now every blade hurts all along its body, handle to tip, as a slider's body does (its middle's way through the step, and the whole blade where it is at the start and the end of it; half as wide as the knife is drawn, `KNIFE_ASPECT`); the stage bullets made from them (other bosses' acts) the same, and the autoplay reaches to the tips. Knife hits there 38 → 50, up to 40 px out. Read "hit like sliders" as all along the body; they are still used up by a hit as other bullets (not a slider body's damage over time - say if that was meant). Stars 3 → 4.
   - **Aya.** Her black feathers have a white rim (new `StageBullet.Rimmed` / `BulletInstance.Rimmed`: the feather picture drawn in white `AYA_FEATHER_RIM` 0.3 of its radius bigger all round under it).
   - **Star Sapphire.** `SAPPHIRE_STEP_DEGREES` 15 → 7.5 (read "frames twice more populated" as twice as many stars along every line; twice as many lines would be the other reading), and her stars are the bosses' soft star (`BulletShape.SoftStar`, Marisa's) instead of the bullets' sharp one. Stars 3 → 4.
   - **Nina.** What leaves the screen over the last new `NINA_RECORD_BEATS` 8 before a beam turns solid (its preview) is kept where it left, unseen - a stage bullet made at once whose time starts as the beam turns solid - and all of it comes back in from there at that moment, flying straight at the beam (as what leaves while a beam hurts does, which stays). Over the last `NINA_WARN_BEATS` 1 before, each flashes gold where it will come in (`NINA_WARN_SIZE` 5 radii, `NINA_WARN_ALPHA` 0.85, twice, brighter the second time). Standing still, 0:55 + 60 s: hits by what came back 25 → 50.
   - Bench (Zenithfall 1:00 + 25 s, autoplay): 0 hits for Remilia, Nina, Aya, Yoshika, Hisami + Yoshika, Remilia + Star Sapphire; Star Sapphire alone 1 (a star of the denser sphere). Descriptions. Not tried in the game.

As every placeholder that gets her own: no beams in a spinner; the spinner's stars are basic bullets (Lyrica, Kurumi) or her own shots (Son Biten's rings, Noroiko's curses, Kogasa's spirals: `own_shots_only`, `no_spinner_stars`).
   - **Lyrica (`Keyboard`).** Every circle and slider of her section (attacks and pauses: "every") comes again `LYRICA_ECHO_BEATS` 4 later as a copy (CloneLayer, as Doremy's and Sagume's: real circles and sliders, hit when due, hurting as the originals, in her red `LYRICA_COPY_COLOUR`), `LYRICA_ECHO_DISTANCE` 2 times as far from the middle of the playfield - its middle kept `LYRICA_EDGE_MARGIN` 40 px inside the screen as it is at 16:9, or most of them would be off it (`CloneLayer.LyricaShift`; a slider keeps its shape), its approach (and every part's) `LYRICA_ECHO_APPROACH` 2 times as long. What each shot is shot again from the echo's places 4 beats later with half the bullets (`Delayed`, `Halved`), and its beams with half the beams, at least one (new `LaserEmitter.Echoed`). Her own shots stay the placeholder's.
   - **Kurumi (`Vampire`).** In her section every slider's rings are gone and it shoots a beam as a whistled slider does; every circle's rings are gone and it shoots a ring of `KURUMI_CIRCLE_BEAMS` 6 straight beams (the first at the player), each fired for `KURUMI_BEAM_BEATS` 4 from where the circle is, so as long as that, flying out (planner). New `VampireAct`: every one of those beams whose end (its last slice) has left the screen by a beam's width shoots a fan of `KURUMI_FAN_BULLETS` 7 basic bullets `KURUMI_FAN_DEGREES` 120 wide back in from where its end crossed the edge, square to it (read "inwards" so), once - when and where worked out from the straight beam, so it is the same whenever time is looked at from; a beam that another boss's ops steer is looked at frame by frame.
   - **Son Biten (`Staff`).** Every slider's beam of her section stands - every slice where it was fired, the whole `SON_HOLD_GROW` 2 times as big round the middle of the slider's path and as thick - until `SON_HOLD_BEATS` 4 after it began (or until it is all fired), then flies on its way as one piece (the way it was aimed as it began, the first beam at the player), spinning round its middle once in `SON_SLIDER_SPIN_BEATS` 8, every next one the other way round, its size and thickness going back over `SON_HOLD_EASE_BEATS` 0.5 (new `LaserEmitter.Held`, `HoldUntil`: a closed form of time, so it hurts, is drawn and is foreseen where it is; Okina's portals follow it step by step, remembered as for beams that ops steer - 28 ms a frame with Okina before that, 7.5 after). With another boss's ops on a beam the ops win and it flies as before. Her own: every `SON_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 4 a ring of `SON_RING_BEAMS` 15 straight beams round her (as Marisa's spark: one straight line from her as it turns), as thick as a slider's, each fired for `SON_BEAM_BEATS` 4 - so that long, flying out - and spinning round her once in `SON_SPIN_BEATS` 16, every next beam of the ring the other way round, so they cross (read "every next laserbeam" as every next beam of a ring; every next ring would be the other reading); in her spinner too.
   - **Noroiko (`Curse`).** New `CurseAct`: every `NOROIKO_EVERY_BEATS` 0.5 of her attacks (and her spinner) two curses, one off to her left and one to her right: 呪 in red beams (eight strokes after the user's picture: 口 on the left, 兄 - 口 over 儿 - on the right), `NOROIKO_CURSE_CIRCLES` 4 circle radii tall, strokes `NOROIKO_STROKE_CIRCLES` 0.12 circle radii thick each side, every stroke hurting like a slider body. For `NOROIKO_HOMING_BEATS` 8 each speeds up evenly from `NOROIKO_SPEED_FROM` 0.5 to `NOROIKO_SPEED_TO` 2.5 times a plain bullet's speed, turning towards the player at most `NOROIKO_TURN_DEGREES` 45 a beat; then it flies straight on out of the screen. Every slider of her section throws one at the player from where it starts instead of its beam (its beams are gone: planner), as big as the slider is at its widest (`NOROIKO_SLIDER_LEAST` 3 .. `NOROIKO_SLIDER_MOST` 10 circle radii). Where a curse goes depends on where the player has been: it is followed in steps of `NOROIKO_STEP_MS` 10 and kept (going back in time takes the steps back); the autoplay foresees it homing on where the player really is.

The playfield draws them so (`orbitObjects`), and what hurts turns with them (`SliderZone.Orbit`, in `FallOffset`). Each stays `KOGASA_HOLD_BEATS` 8 after it is over, still turning, red if it turns clockwise and blue if not (`KOGASA_RED` / `KOGASA_BLUE`; new `SurpriseAct`, shown by the FallLayer as Ariya's held ones are - it now takes a colour), hurting as a slider body does (its zone lasts as long; a circle gets a zone of one point from when it is due). 563. **(2026-11-24) Kogasa's umbrella; Son Biten's beams spin round their own middles.** The user (with a photo of a purple paper umbrella with an eye and a tongue): "This umbrella, clear background, generate missing parts, for Kogasa, spins on the middle too. Finished osu objects keep spinning / Son biten - laserbeams move normally but also spin around their own centres".
   - **Kogasa's umbrella.** New `Tools/Bake umbrella <photo> <out.png> <size> <preview.png>`: the photo was taken a little from the side (the knob, the umbrella's true middle, is right of the middle of its outline), its rim runs off the picture on the left and a red cloth hides it at the bottom left. It is unwrapped round the knob into a circle: along every way out from the knob its rim is the first purple met from outside in (past the mouth too); where that is hidden (the umbrella runs off the picture, or the cloth lies right outside it: 109 of 720 ways) its distance is filled in from the ways on either side and smoothed, and the outer part of a hidden way is taken from just outside its hidden stretch, reflected across the nearer end of it (seamless there and lit alike; first tried from the mirror image across the knob's upright line, lighter there, and from the nearest way seen, which repeated it in stripes), blended in from inside; the edge soft. `Resources/Textures/kogasa_umbrella.png` 512 x 512 (`StageTexture.KogasaUmbrella`, in `stageBack`). In her section it lies in the middle of the playfield, under everything, `KOGASA_UMBRELLA_SIZE` 240 px across, `KOGASA_UMBRELLA_ALPHA` 0.9, turning clockwise as her circles and sliders do (`KOGASA_ORBIT_DEGREES` 15 a beat), from her first beat until the last of hers is gone, fading in and out over half a beat (`SurpriseAct.Draw`). It only shows.
   - "Finished osu objects keep spinning": as they did - over their eight beats after they are over they go on turning round the middle (read so; that they should turn round their own middles too, as the umbrella does, would be the other reading).
   - **Son Biten.** Her own beams were straight lines from her turning round her (as Marisa's spark); now each is thrown: fired for `SON_BEAM_BEATS` 4 from where she is as it begins (it stays there), it flies out as any beam does and turns round its own middle - the slice halfway between its first and its newest - once in `SON_SPIN_BEATS` 16, every next one of a ring the other way round (new `LaserEmitter.SelfSpin`, a closed form of time; `FliesStraightAlone` false for it and for the held ones, so Okina's portals follow them step by step). Her held slider beams already turned round their own middles as they flew (562). Bench (Zenithfall 1:00 + 25 s): autoplay 2.25 ms a frame, 1 hit (a star; 3 by her beams before); standing still 43 hits by beams; with Okina (0:20 + 40 s, `PORTAL_LENGTH`) 0 stretched, 4.5 ms. Description.
564. **(2026-11-24) Eirin twice as many constellations; Mugetsu slower and faster round; arrows for Kogasa's and Son Biten's spins; four placeholders get mechanics of their own: Iku, Meira, Narumi, Parsee.** The user: "Eirin Yatagoro - double the constellations amount / Mugetsu - halve the bullets speed but double the spin speed / Iku Nagae - ... / Meira - ... / Narumi Yatadera - ... / Parsee Mizuhashi - ... / Also arrow warnings for Kogasa spin, Biten spin", then (on Iku being heavy) "iku mechanics stay but skip every second bullet".
   - **Eirin.** A bullet could be in one constellation at most, and the bullets on the screen were all there was to join (on Zenithfall about 50 constellations a mark, 74 % of what was looked at joined): a mark every four beats instead of eight was tried and made no more of them in eight beats (56). Now what a constellation lets go - let go as the next preview begins - may stand in the next one: on the next mark's first batch the act's own flying bullets are looked at too, where they will be when it stops, and the stop is put on their legs as the constellation is made (`ConstellationAct.standAgain`; they fly four beats and stand four again). About 105 a mark against 50 (`EIRIN_EVERY_BEATS` stays 8; the comment says what was tried). A mark looks at a bullet even while another mark looks (`looks`, the marks may overlap), and one the mark before has just stopped is left out. New `BulletField.ConstellationProbe` (`CONSTELLATION_PROBE=1` on `field`: made, left, looked at, every batch). Autoplay 0 hits, 2.4 ms a frame. Stars 3 -> 4. Description.
   - **Mugetsu.** In her attacks every bullet on the screen flies half as fast: a window of the bullets' clock (`BossEffects`, `MUGETSU_SLOWED` 0.5, as Mystia's, without the flashlight); her vortex takes her attacks by that clock (`Swirl.Clock`, the field's effects: the attack begins and ends for a bullet when its clock says, so it goes round no longer than the attack). `MUGETSU_SWIRL` 2 -> 8: going round her goes with how far a bullet gets, so at half the speed 8 is twice as fast round her as before (read so: 4 would be as fast round her as before, twice as many turns on the way out). Autoplay 0 hits, 2.0 ms; standing still 301 hits (273). Description.

566. **(2026-11-25) Four placeholders get mechanics of their own: Sara (`Gatekeeper`), Mai Teireida (`Bamboo`), Satono Nishida (`Myoga`), Takane Yamashiro (`Satellites`).** The user's list. As with every placeholder that gets a mechanic, none of them has the spinner's beams, and their spinners shoot their own bullets (the planner's sets: own shots only, their own spinner bullets, none of the spinner's stars).
   - **Sara** ("Gradually decrease bullets speed from 2x to 0.5x from her to playfield edge. She herself every 16 beats shoots two thick instant straight laserbeams (master spark sound and shake should play) to her sides and they both close into 87 degrees during 8 beats then fade out. 4 beat preview before spawn. She herself shoot ring of gapless bullets every 2 beats (but there is a gap every 4th bullet in the ring)").
     - Every ring fired in her section (anyone's; not one that ricochets, falls, homes, goes round once, is a big sharp ball or a train, nor one that slows down already) flies `SARA_FAST` 2 times its speed right by her and slows evenly to `SARA_SLOW` 0.5 of it at the edge of the screen ahead of it. A bullet starts as fast as where it comes out says (how far from her, of the way from her out to that edge) and slows evenly from there (new `BulletEmitter.SlowsFromBoss`, beside Futo's and Ran's `SlowsToWall`; where she stands is taken as the ring is fired, `SnapSlowCentre`). Read so; a bullet flying towards her does not speed up again as it nears her (the other reading: a speed by where it is at every moment).
     - Her beams (`GateAct`): every `SARA_EVERY_BEATS` 16 a see-through preview for `SARA_PREVIEW_BEATS` 4, then two beams `SARA_BEAM_CIRCLES` 1 circle radius from their middle line to each side come out of her at once, whole, past the edge of the screen. They point to her left and her right, square to the line from her to the player as it was when the preview came, and close in towards the player evenly over `SARA_CLOSE_BEATS` 8 until they are `SARA_CLOSED_DEGREES` 87 apart. Then they fade over `SARA_FADE_BEATS` 1, harmless by then.
     - The beams hurt like a slider body; the Master Spark's sound plays and the screen shakes (`SARA_SHAKE` 0.5) while they are solid. She stands still from the preview until they have faded.
     - Her rings: every `SARA_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 2, basic bullets out of the edge of her magic circle, as many as lie there body against body (44 at CS 4), every `SARA_RING_GAP_EVERY` 4th left out. Every second ring is turned half a gap round, so the gaps do not line up (read so; gaps that stay in line would be the other reading). They keep all their bullets with another boss on the screen.
   - **Mai Teireida** ("Stays and Shoots 36 instant slider laserbeams length reaching playfield edge with 8 beat preview, duration is 8 beats, she does that every 16 beats, no pause phases. Also every bullet has 2x speed but every 4 beats moves 45 degree left out of its normal direction then stops for 4 beats, then moves 45 degree right from her normal direction then stops then repeats. She herself shoots circular ring of 30 talismans every 2 beats that follow same sequence").
     - No pauses.
     - She stays where she is when her section begins (`BambooAct.BossAt`).
     - Her beams: every `TEIREIDA_EVERY_BEATS` 16, `TEIREIDA_BEAMS` 36 beams as thin as a slider's, from her out past the edge of the screen, evenly round her. The player, as they were when the preview came, is in the middle of a gap. A see-through preview lasts `TEIREIDA_PREVIEW_BEATS` 8; then the beams are there at once and hurt like a slider body for `TEIREIDA_LIVE_BEATS` 8, fading over `TEIREIDA_FADE_BEATS` 0.25 as the next preview comes.
     - Every bullet fired in her section moves by its own clock (new op `TrajectoryOpKind.Zigzag`, which `BulletEmitter.ConfigureOps` makes into new `Turn`s and freezes, as Aunn's): `TEIREIDA_SPEED` 2 times as fast as it was fired, `TEIREIDA_TURN_DEGREES` 45 to the left of its way, for `TEIREIDA_MOVE_BEATS` 4; it stands `TEIREIDA_STOP_BEATS` 4; it moves 45 to the right of its way; it stands; and so on until her section is over (then its last move goes on).
     - The sequence goes by each bullet's own clock, read so; one beat clock for every bullet at once is the other reading.

   - **Satono Nishida** ("Every bullet that crossed top then moves bottom. Every bullet that crossed bottom, including ones which fallen from top also releases a talisman that initially aimed at player and does not changes direction. She herself shoots marisa sliders that spawn randomly within her magic circle every 0.25 beats, each one stops for 2 beats then initially chooses a direction parallel to between boss and player line"). Her act is `MyogaAct`.
     - All through her section, a bullet of a ring that goes out of the top comes back down from where it left, straight down at the speed it had, once (as Toyohime's drop; not one that ricochets, falls or homes).
     - Every bullet of a ring that goes out of the bottom, one that came back down too, leaves a talisman where it left, as big as a boss's star. The talisman flies straight at the player as they are then, at `SATONO_TALISMAN_SPEED` 1 times a plain bullet's speed, and never turns; talismans themselves are not turned or copied.

It stands `SATONO_STAR_STAND_BEATS` 2, then flies off along the line from her to the player as it is then: parallel to that line, not at the player, at `SATONO_STAR_SPEED` 1. Her stars come back down from the top and leave talismans at the bottom too. "Marisa sliders" was read as Marisa's stars.

567. **(2026-11-25) Sara: the line her beams close in on is chosen once.** The user: "laserbeams close in line is choosen initially when sequency starts, it does not change dynamically". It already was: each pair takes the line from her to the player when its preview comes (`GateAct.aim`) and closes in on it to the end, the next pair taking a new one (only a seek back past a preview takes it again). Her description now says so ("one to each side of the line from her to where you were when the preview came ... close in on that line, which does not move"); it read as if the beams followed the player.
568. **(2026-11-25) The user's long list: two lags, Suika's lava and impact frame, and 22 bosses tuned.** The user's list, with six screenshots (saved with the list in `Danmakusu\Screenshots\2026-11-25 round 568\`: "Save screenshots to Danmakusu to prevent losing context").
    - **Kokoro's lag** ("Fix Kokoro Lag"): every beam of hers went slice by slice through some forty of her ops (an aim or pull a beat, falls, ricochets), all from where the slice was fired, every frame, at every point the bend search tried (about 97 traces a beam a pass).
      - New `TraceCarry` (Trajectory.cs): `Trajectory.Trace` keeps how far a slice has got through the ops that are over by the field's time and can no longer change (`settles`: aims and pulls once placed, falls, ricochets that keep no flashes, turns, coasts) and goes on from there next time. `LaserEmitter` keeps a carry per slice (by fire time, start and way; what was not asked for since the frame before is let go); the field sets `LaserEmitter.SettledTime` every frame; time going back (`rewindStage`) throws every carry away (`TraceCarry.CurrentGeneration`). Not for a beam on Gengetsu's clock.
      - `LaserEmitter.leftTheScreen` is skipped for a beam with no op that could bring a slice back (`bringsBack`).
      - Bench, Touhou Last Boss Rush (Extra Stage) 11:20 + 20 s, Always Boss, Kokoro alone: 38.1 → 10.3 ms a frame; with the autoplay 50.0 → about 22. The beams are the same: as many points and traces as before, to the one.
    - **Shion's lag** ("Very strong lag with Shion"). The game's log (the 13:11 autoplay of the Boss Rush, 124 bosses, double, Always Boss): from 4:50.7 to the end of her section 17,255 rings, 70-79 ms a frame.
      - At her first mark the planner copies every ring whose lifetime goes over it; nearly all of them had left the screen long before (most were Yorihime's and Seiran's burst of 4:25), and the field hid every bullet of those copies but kept the rings to the end. Now a ring made out of other bullets (`DeriveKind.Explode`) whose every bullet is hidden is gone at once (`ringGone`), and a hidden bullet of a copy stands where a shown one is (it stood at 0, 0, which kept the ring longer). Bench of the same game, 4:51: 259 rings (17,255 in the game).
      - A beam whose ops only turn it round or mirror it (hers) no longer looks for bends between every two points: there are none between two slices that went through the same ops, and where an op's moment lies between two, a point is put on either side of it (`LaserEmitter.affineOnly`). Shion alone: 19.4 → 12.5 ms a frame, no long segments.
    - **Suika**:
      - "Why is magma texture inconsistently splitting and stretching": the lava picture was laid along each crack, as big as that crack was wide, so it was sheared across the pieces at every jag and of another size on every crack. It is now laid flat over the ground, `SUIKA_LAVA_TILE_CIRCLES` 9 circle radii wide (the big crack's old size), and repeats both ways (`StageLayer.makeLava` goes round in y too; `sectioncheck lava` draws it 2 x 2). Every piece is cut where the picture starts again; `MeshQuad` has a place on the picture for every corner (`UvA`..`UvD`, the `Uv` rectangle is gone) and the renderer draws the lava through a quad batch of its own that sets each corner's place.
      - "Why outlines are not same thickness": the crust was 13 % of the half width; now `SUIKA_CRUST_CIRCLES` 0.1 circle radius on every crack, branch and twig (where a crack is no wider, it is all crust).
      - "Why branches are not sharp": the branches and twigs of the big crack and the stomp's cracks' branches narrow to nothing (they ended at 20 % and 15 % of their width, round). Read as the branches; the cracks themselves still end round. All ends sharp is the other reading.
      - `SUIKA_STARS_BEFORE_BEATS` 2 → 1.
      - "Explosion impact frame area edges are not smooth": the shapes of the impact frame are hard-edged pieces, so their edges fell between the screen's pixels in steps. New `ImpactStarLayer.AddOutline`: a strip 1.5 screen px wide outside a shape's outline, fully turned at its edge and not at all at its outer edge (the colour is not premultiplied by the renderer, so it goes down with the alpha). Suika's band (both sides and round ends) and every impact star (the World's Ender's too) have one.
    - **Aya**: her feathers turn as they fly, the two of a bullet opposite ways (`AYA_FEATHER_SPIN` 0.006 rad/ms); `AYA_FEATHER_RIM` 0.3 → 0.15.
    - **Star Sapphire**: `SAPPHIRE_STAR_SIZE` 2 → 1; `SAPPHIRE_GROW_BEATS`, `_HOLD_`, `_BACK_` 8 → 16 ("sphere phase doubled" read as every part of it; only the growing is the other reading). As each sphere has grown another is thrown over it from her, turning the other way, never pulled back: it grows on as fast as the first grew until it is wholly past the screen (`SphereAct.addSphere`, `thrownAt`; the petals' sound with it).
    - **Mai Teireida**: Byakuren's beam sound as her beams come; she stands still only during the preview and moves on once they are shot, and they stay where they were shot (`BambooAct` keeps where each set came from); `TEIREIDA_FADE_BEATS` 0.25 → 1, as the set ends; for `TEIREIDA_WARN_BEATS` 2 before each move after a stop a red arrow beside every bullet points the way it will go (the zigzag's turns carry a warning, `TrajectoryOp.TurnWarn`; `BulletEmitter.GoTurns`; red turned arrows in `SwellWarnings`).
    - **Sara**: `SARA_CLOSED_DEGREES` 87 → 6 (three degrees to either side of the line); `SARA_SLOW` 0.5 → 0.25 (Futo's "same as for newest boss who had that mechanic", read as Sara getting it too).
    - **Son Biten**: a slider's beam already on the screen as her section begins spins from then on too (`LaserEmitter.SpinFrom`, `lateSpun`; `StaffAct` shows its arrows from then for a beat).
    - **Ellen**: `ELLEN_BEAM_SOLID_BEATS` 1 → 2.
    - **Merlin**: the dim leaves the circles and sliders themselves alone, fades in and out with its place over `MERLIN_DIM_FADE_MS` 250 and darkens in `MERLIN_DIM_STEPS` 8 layers (3); when an attack ends her white rings stop where they are and fade out there over `MERLIN_RING_FADE_BEATS` 1, harmless (they went on out of the screen).
    - **"Those vertical and horizontal laserbeams appeared out of nowhere"** (screenshot 3, 4:34 of that game: Yorihime and Seiran ending, Rumia and Mai beginning): not found. The bench of the same 124 bosses, double, Always Boss, with the song, 4:05-4:42, with the autoplay and with the probe's moving player, draws no long slider or boss beam piece but Mai's snowflake's arms (long by design). Not changed.
    - **Yoshika**: her talismans fly straight back the way they came ("remove spinning": they went round a big loop and a small one first, since 2026-11-22).
    - **Ubame**: `UBAME_DOLL_SPEED` 1.2 → 2.4.
    - **Takane**: her big balls are her green (white balls with their white glow looked blurred - "low resolution bullets"); `TAKANE_ORBIT_BEATS` 2 → 8; a grabbed bullet goes round as far out as the big one is big and an osu! circle radius more, easing out to there over half a beat (it stayed as far as it was grabbed; `StageBullet.OrbitFrom`).
    - **Kanako**: `KANAKO_GROW_BEATS` 2 → 8; two gaps a talisman wide across from each other (every fourth talisman was left out); `KANAKO_RING_TURN_BEATS` 16 → 32; when a circle stops turning its talismans fly off straight out from its middle at `KANAKO_FLY_SPEED` 1 plain bullet's speed (and the circle's way), a red arrow beside each `KANAKO_WARN_BEATS` 2 before.
    - **Mugetsu**: `MUGETSU_SLOWED` 0.5 → 1 (normal speed), `MUGETSU_SWIRL` 8 → 2 (half the linear spin at the full speed). "Spin applies to bullets that were already on the screen too": the rings on the screen as an attack begins already went round her, so this is read as the acts' own bullets that fly straight (Kanako's talismans flying off, the prism's spread...): they go round her too now (`swirlStage`, drawing, hits and the autoplay). Beams going round her is the other reading.
    - **Futo**: `FUTO_SPEED_AT_WALL` 0.3 → 0.25.
    - **Keiki**: `KEIKI_BEAM_BEATS` 4 → 2; every bullet comes back as two beams, `KEIKI_BEAM_SPLIT_DEGREES` 90 apart, 45 to either side of the way back.
    - **Sunny Milk**: every bullet the prism splits has a narrow beam of its colour for a tail, `SUNNY_TAIL_BEATS` 2 of its way long, `SUNNY_TAIL_WIDTH` 0.6 of its radius narrowing to a point; it hurts like a slider body.
    - **Mima**: once a moon has faded its body shoots out `MIMA_BODY_STARS` 40 white soft stars (Marisa's) from places all over it, by a hash, each a way of its own, at `MIMA_BODY_SPEED` 0.5 plain bullet's speed, half its edges' ("randomly filled" read as from all over the body).
    - **Narumi**: her waves give twice the speed a bullet has now (twice its own, before): from her quarter it goes to half its own.
    - **Kogasa**: her umbrella turns half as fast as her objects (`KOGASA_UMBRELLA_TURN` 0.5), `KOGASA_UMBRELLA_SIZE` 120 → 60, and hurts like a slider body; an arrow fades out `KOGASA_ARROW_BEATS` 1 after its circle or slider has come (it stayed until that was gone).
    - **Utsuho**: `UTSUHO_JET_TURN_FACTOR` 0.125 → 0.25, `UTSUHO_JET_MAX_TURN` a quarter → half a turn (twice as fast for as long).
    - **Hatate**: her frame stays where it is, with its grey glass, `HATATE_FRAME_STAY_BEATS` 2 after the picture; only what is in the frame as the picture is taken turns (`TrajectoryOp.InFrameOnly`, `FrameCentre`/`FrameHalf`/`FrameAngle`).
    - **Noroiko**: the bench draws her curses and they hit (the screenshot, 11:21, is before her first throw at about 11:24). Thin and white in the middle they passed for slider beams: now red all through (`addPlainBeam` takes a core colour) and `NOROIKO_STROKE_CIRCLES` 0.12 → 0.24.
    - **Parsee**: her flowers are where the ball really is, by the bullets' clock (with Gengetsu's they were where it would be by the game's time).
    - **Iku**: the five of a group are joined as a five-pointed star (a chain in ring order before), gold all through, `IKU_LINK_WIDTH` 0.1 → 0.2.
    - **Shinki**: every bullet comes out of two wings in turn (`SHINKI_BULLET_COPIES` 2), `SHINKI_STREAM_GAP_BEATS` 1/16 → 1/32, her beams `SHINKI_BEAM_LONGER` 4 times as long.
    - Stars: Sunny Milk 3 → 4, Hatate 3 → 2, Shinki 4 → 5. Descriptions and short lines.
    - Bench, Boss Rush 11:20 + 14 s, Always Boss, the song, autoplay, `AUTO_BUDGET=100`, each alone, no errors: ms a frame Suika 7.4, Star Sapphire 2.2, Mai Teireida 4.4, Sara 2.5, Son Biten 3.3, Ellen 2.1, Merlin 2.2, Yoshika 9.2, Ubame 4.1, Takane 2.8, Kanako 17.6, Mugetsu 2.2, Futo 8.6, Keiki 3.1, Sunny 10.3, Mima 41.2, Narumi 6.3, Kogasa 3.7, Utsuho 9.1, Hatate 3.3, Noroiko 2.7, Parsee 12.3, Iku 9.3, Shinki 8.8, Aya 2.1, Mugetsu + Kanako 22.3.

569. **(2026-11-25) Alice's beams turn out, Aya's preview twice as long, Sunny's tails.** The user's list.
    - **Alice** ("doll laserbeams small angle to left and right"): the beam on either side of a stream flies `ALICE_BEAM_TURN_DEGREES` 10 out from the stream's way, to its own side (they flew alongside it). Read as turning out; turning in towards the stream is the other reading.
    - **Aya** ("laserbeam preview doubled"): `AYA_PREVIEW_LEAST_BEATS` 0.25 → 0.75. With her dashes on the half beats a preview was 0.25-0.75 of a beat (half a beat on the whole); now 0.75-1.25 (a beat). Read as how long it shows; twice as thick or as bright is the other reading.
    - **Sunny Milk**:
      - "For some reason traces disappear early": when a stage bullet leaves the screen the field ends its life then (`drawStageBullets` sets `Until`), and a tail was drawn only while its bullet lived, so the whole tail went the moment its bullet left, most of it still on the screen. Now it goes on out after it, from where it left, for a tail's length (`PrismAct.tailOf`: its front stays where the bullet left; it still hurts there).
      - "Traces body colors are rainbow too": a tail's middle was white, as a boss's beam's; now it is its colour too, lighter (`SUNNY_TAIL_MIDDLE_LIGHT` 0.45 of the way to white).
    - Descriptions. Bench, Boss Rush 11:20 + 14 s, autoplay, each alone: 0 hits; Alice 6.4, Aya 1.9, Sunny 11.5 ms a frame.
570. **(2026-11-25) Boss sections: an attack is never longer than the boss's own.** The user: "boss arranger - some sections are not getting sliced into attacks - attack duration is too long even if boss count is big".
    - Since 2026-11-01 (229) what was left of a boss's piece after its last whole attack and pause went to its last attack, so that nothing was shorter than 16 beats. With many bosses the pieces are rarely whole numbers of attacks and pauses: on the Boss Rush (Extra Stage) with the user's 124 bosses, double, Always Boss, every piece is about 47 beats, and every boss had one attack of 31 beats and one pause of 16.
    - Now (a boss who pauses) an attack is at most the boss's own (`burst`): what is left after the last whole attack and pause is one more attack, as long as it is, when it is at least half an attack, otherwise the last pause has it. That piece of 47 beats is now attack 16, pause 16, attack 15. Attacks can be 8-16 beats at the end of a piece.
    - A boss who never pauses keeps one attack for the whole piece (32 beats and what is left, as before): read as being about the bosses who attack and pause; cutting their time into attacks too is the other reading.
    - `sectioncheck lengths`: `ALWAYS_BOSS=1`, `LENGTHS_AT_ONCE=2|3` (double, triple), a third pool `all` (the whole roster), the longest attack, and `LENGTHS_LONGEST=1` names who has attacks longer than the default. The Boss Rush, Always Boss, double, 150 bosses: longer than 16 beats only Merlin and Utsuho (32, theirs are twice as long) and the bosses without pauses (22-34).

    - **Kogasa** ("Rollback doubling bullets on Kogasa"): `KOGASA_SHOTS_FACTOR` 2 → 1, `KOGASA_MOST_SHOTS` 96 → 48, as before 2026-11-24 (entry of that day: "double the bullets amount coming out of spinning objects"). Her staying circles and sliders shoot what they had, one at a time, over their eight beats. The Boss Rush plans 89,275 rings with her (158,391).
    - **Kanako**: `KANAKO_CIRCLE_RADII` 5 → 2.5 ("halve the circles radius"; as before 2026-11-21, fewer talismans to a circle as they lie end to end), `KANAKO_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 8 ("halve the frequency of circles shooting"), `KANAKO_FLY_SPEED` 1 → 0.5 ("talismans fly away in half speed").
    - Descriptions. Bench, Boss Rush 11:20 + 14 s, autoplay: Kogasa 0 hits, 2.6 ms a frame; Kanako 0 hits, 6.0 ms (17.6).
572. **(2026-11-25) Three new bosses (Tewi, Sumireko, Orange), the impact frame's soft edges wider, and six bosses tuned.** The user's list.
    - **Tewi Inaba** (new `BossMechanic.Hare`, `HareAct`; a placeholder until now): "4 big laserbeams (two left and two right with preview) spawned initially. Then they turn into two parallel laserbeams left and right to tewi, parallel to line between tewi and player initially. While laserbeam is on (for 16 beats) tewi stays on place."
      - Every `TEWI_EVERY_BEATS` 32: four beams `TEWI_BEAM_CIRCLES` 0.8 circle radii to each side of their middle line, an X round the line from her to the player as it is when the preview comes (`TEWI_SPREAD_DEGREES` 45, two ahead, two behind); a see-through preview of `TEWI_PREVIEW_BEATS` 2, then solid with Byakuren's beam sound (`touhou_strike`); over `TEWI_SWING_BEATS` 4 the two of each side turn (the one ahead to the line, the one behind to its way back) and slide out until they are one beam `TEWI_OFFSET_CIRCLES` 3 circle radii to her side: two parallel beams across the screen, her between them. Solid `TEWI_LIVE_BEATS` 16 in all, then they fade over `TEWI_FADE_BEATS` 0.5. They hurt like a slider body; she stands still from the preview until they are gone.
      - Read "4 big laserbeams two left and two right" as an X round the line to the player; four beams fanned to her sides is the other reading. Read "for 16 beats" as their whole solid time; 16 beats after the swing is the other.
    - **Sumireko Usami** (`BossMechanic.Psychokinesis`, planned only until now, `PsychokinesisAct`): "Drops 4x size laserbeam like matenshi, with previews and such. Once active slowly moves left or right depending on where is player. Glows, blooms, dim around. Movement continues for 8 beats then it explodes into a lot of marisa stars (thrown left and right) with suika impact frames. She shoots one laserbeam every 16 beats. While active marisa laserbeam sound and shake plays."
      - Every `SUMIREKO_EVERY_BEATS` 16 a beam comes down from the top as Matenshi's do, `SUMIREKO_BEAM_CIRCLES` 12 circle radii wide (read as four times Matenshi's 3; four times a slider's beam is the other reading), over where the player is when its preview comes (`SUMIREKO_PREVIEW_BEATS` 4, see-through). Solid, it reaches the bottom in a quarter beat, blooms (`SUMIREKO_BLOOM` 0.8: plain beams take a bloom now, `addPlainBeam(..., bloom)`), the screen dims round it (Miko's dim, `SUMIREKO_DIM` 0.55), the Master Spark's sound and a shake (`SUMIREKO_SHAKE` 0.5) go on.
      - For `SUMIREKO_MOVE_BEATS` 8 it drifts `SUMIREKO_DRIFT_CIRCLES` 0.5 circle radii a beat towards the side the player is on as it turns solid (read "depending on where is player" as towards; decided once, so it is a closed form); then it explodes (Hecatia's explosion) into `SUMIREKO_STARS` 64 of Marisa's soft stars from all over it, the left half thrown left and the right half right, at 0.6-1.6 a plain bullet's speed, with Suika's impact frame for `SUMIREKO_IMPACT_MS` 840 (its band, a little wider than it, turned inside out with the soft edge, and `SUMIREKO_IMPACT_STARS` 8 impact stars beside it, in flashes). It hurts like a slider body while solid.
    - **Orange** (new `BossMechanic.Citrus`, `CitrusAct`; a placeholder until now): "always on centre - during warning orange grows into 4x osu circle orange.png, then 8 beats later it splits and flies away, arrow warning. While flies away another orange grows. It also splits away but also slices that left return to form another orange".
      - The user's `orange.png` (now `Resources/Textures/orange.png`) is cut into one slice at load (`StageTexture.OrangeSlice`, `StageLayer.makeOrangeSlice`: the top 36 degrees of the fruit, found by what is neither white nor see-through, with a pale pith line along both cut edges and a pale core - the picture's own lines are not where ten even slices cut it - and a pixel past its edges so two slices side by side have no seam; `sectioncheck orange` draws it and the ten together).
      - She stands in the middle (`bossAtCentre`, `BossAt`). The orange is `ORANGE_RADIUS_CIRCLES` 4 circle radii from its middle to its rind (read "4x osu circle" so). It grows see-through in her warning (over `ORANGE_GROW_BEATS` 4 without one), stands `ORANGE_HOLD_BEATS` 8, then its `ORANGE_SLICES` 10 slices fly straight out past the farthest corner of the screen (`ORANGE_BEYOND_CIRCLES` 2 more) and back over `ORANGE_FLIGHT_BEATS` 16 (fast near the middle, slow far out), meet as the orange and fly out again at once. A second orange grows in the middle while the first's slices fly out and splits as they turn back: from then on one orange's slices come back as the other's go. For `ORANGE_WARN_BEATS` 2 before every split a red arrow on every slice points its way. Slices and a standing orange hurt like a slider body (a wedge each); a growing one does not. After her section what has split flies on out.
    - Tewi, Sumireko and Orange: their spinners shoot their own bullets without the spinner's beams (`own_spinner_bullets`); their basic attack stays the placeholder's spirals. Stars: Tewi 3, Sumireko 4, Orange 3. Descriptions.
    - **The impact frame's soft edge** ("white impact frames edges are still not smooth, why??? ... Fix fix fix until impact frame white area edges are gradually smooth"): the strip of 1.5 screen px was too narrow to be seen as a gradient. `ImpactStarLayer.AddOutline` takes a width per outline: `IMPACT_BAND_FEATHER_PX` 14 round Suika's band (and Sumireko's), `IMPACT_STAR_FEATHER_PX` 5 round every impact star (their tails are thin; the World's Ender's too).
    - **Aunn** ("every bullet can stop at most 4 times"): `AUNN_MOST_STOPS` 4, the stand as it is fired the first (new `TrajectoryOp.MostStops`); then its last move goes on (it stopped until her section was over). Counting only the stops after moves is the other reading.
    - **Star Sapphire**: the sphere that grows away is thrown with the sphere itself, as it begins ("both ... comes out at the same time"; it was thrown once that had grown), and its stars are white (`TINT_WHITE` on its beads). One sound for both.

    - **Mugetsu** ("arrows warn about spin"): for `MUGETSU_WARN_BEATS` 2 before each attack a gold arrow beside two of every four bullets of a ring that goes round her (one of each way round) points the way it will go: its own way plus round her, by its sign, as `Swirl.Apply` (`addMugetsuArrow`, worked out with the bullets as Kokoro's; the act says where she is).
    - **Ellen** ("Socrates - can jump on any bullet, including bosses"): the cat chose among the rings' bullets only (the bosses' rings among them); now the bosses' own bullets too - every act's (Kanako's talismans, Alice's streams, Sunny's splits...: `FamiliarAct.Seat`, a ring's bullet or an act's).
    - **Noroiko** ("her bullets and laserbeams still don't form that japanese symbol, it is just basic bullet for some reason"): the bench draws her curses in the very game of the screenshot of round 568 (Noroiko and Kokoro, 11:21-11:29: 211,320 strokes in 8 s, `ACT_BEAMS=1`; her first attack there began at 11:21.9, after the screenshot), and osu!'s log of tonight's plays shows her act at work, without errors. Read as the rings of her section: every ring of the map's bullets in it is laid out as 呪 now (new `RingShape.Curse`, `CurseGlyph.STROKES` shared with her curses; bullets along the strokes by their length, two at least on each, `NOROIKO_RING_LEAST` 40 at least; turned with a ring that aims). If the curses themselves are not seen in the game, that is still open.
    - Tools: `ACT_BEAMS=1` on `field` counts the beams every act draws; `PERIODS_ATTACKS=1` on `periods` prints every boss's attacks; `sectioncheck orange <slice.png> <whole.png>`.

573. **(2026-11-26) Three new bosses (Yuiman Asama, Yachie Kicchou, Rikako Asakura); Kogasa's objects turn, Ariya's second grid, Sara's and Tewi's pairs of beams seamless; Orange's orange is drawn at last.** The user's list, with six pictures (saved with the request in `Screenshots\2026-11-26 round 573`).
    - **Kogasa** ("osu objects themselves turn around center too"): her circles and sliders went round the middle of the playfield keeping their own way up; now each turns as one piece with its turn (`SurpriseOrbit.Turn.Place` / `Unplace` / `Degrees`, a rotation round the middle): the playfield sets their `Rotation` (`orbitObjects`), the ones that stay turn on the `FallLayer` too (its entries take an angle), the slider zones turn the character back before they check it (`SliderZone.Local`), and the planner shoots from the turned places (the slider beams' ball positions too). Read as every object turning with its place; spinning on its own besides is the other reading.
    - **Ariya** ("grids half the size, but another grid that moves horizontally too"): her ellipses are half the size (`ARIYA_ELLIPSE_SIZE` 1.33 → 0.665), the gaps as they were (new `ARIYA_GRID_SIZE` 1.33 bullet radii, times `ARIYA_COLUMN_GAP`); a second grid comes in from the left at the same moments, columns down the whole screen, every second one half a gap aside, each ellipse bouncing once off the right side and flying back (`FossilAct.column`; its ellipses are an act of their own, `FossilColumnsAct`, as they live longer than the rows). Read "half the size" as her ellipses. The whole grid half the size (gaps halved too) was built first: three times the ellipses, 14 ms a frame without the autoplay and 41 with it; that is the other reading.
    - **Sara and Tewi** ("double laserbeams are seamless, for Tewi too"): each pair is drawn as one beam from the end of one to the end of the other through where they meet (Sara's through her, Tewi's through each side's meeting point); they were two beams lying over each other there. How they hurt is unchanged.
    - **Orange**: her slice's picture was in no layer's list (since 572), so her orange was drawn without a picture - not seen at all. Now in `stageBack`'s when she is on the map.
    - **Yuiman Asama** (new `BossMechanic.Pinwheel`, `PinwheelAct` and `PinwheelArrowsAct`; a placeholder until now): "All bullets on the screen, also additionally her bullets shoot from 4 spinning points in those dissentered arcs. Additionally she shoots 10 arrowhead (non-homing) bullets that shoot randomly basic bullets to left and right (something like Parsee but bullets leave immediately)".
      - `YUIMAN_POINTS` 4 points - red, yellow, green, blue - turn round her `YUIMAN_POINT_CIRCLES` 1.5 circle radii out, once every `YUIMAN_TURN_BEATS` 8. Every bullet fired in her section (what Shinki's wings take: not Utsuho's big balls, the train or what homes) comes out of a point instead, the four in turn, two side by side (`YUIMAN_PAIR_GAP` 2.4 of their radius apart), `YUIMAN_AIM_DEGREES` 60 from straight out the way the points turn, at its own speed, in the point's colour (`YUIMAN_TINT_AMOUNT` 0.85); a point's pairs of one ring `YUIMAN_STREAM_GAP_BEATS` 1/16 apart. The streams curl into arcs that miss her middle.
      - Every `YUIMAN_ARROW_EVERY_BEATS` 8 of her attacks a ring of `YUIMAN_ARROWS` 10 arrowheads, one at the player, at `YUIMAN_ARROW_SPEED` 0.5 a plain bullet's speed, straight; each lets out a basic bullet (`YUIMAN_DROP_SIZE` 0.6) to its left or its right about every `YUIMAN_DROP_EVERY_BEATS` 0.25 (side and moment by a hash), square to its way give or take `YUIMAN_DROP_SPREAD_DEGREES` 25, flying off at once at `YUIMAN_DROP_SPEED` 1.
      - Read "all bullets on the screen" as every bullet fired in her section coming out of the points, as Shinki's wings do; only her own shots doing so is the other reading. Her own shots are only the arrowheads (`own_shots_only`): with the placeholder's spirals besides, twice as much went through the points, and so much that the cap on an act's bullets (60,000) was reached and the rest was not shown.
      - The arrowheads are an act of their own: they live long, and an act's bullets are passed over as gone only from the first one on, so behind them every stream bullet was looked at every frame (9.7 → 2.4 ms a frame, without the autoplay).
    - **Yachie Kicchou** (new `BossMechanic.Cubes`, `CubesAct`; a placeholder until now): "Shoots randomly a lot of rings of ...arrowhead (no homing) rings that grow inwards to the centre and outwards repeatedly (3x osu circle radius each circle, moving inwards 4 beats, outwards 4 beats), arrowhead body turns clockwise or counterclockwise. On top she spawns big cube pattern laserbeams that are initially transparent and harmless, but it activates outwards, and deactivates also outwards like Mai."
      - No pauses (`no_pauses`, `own_shots_only`, `no_spinner_stars`). Every `YACHIE_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 1 a ring of `YACHIE_RING_ARROWS` 12 arrowheads from her, each ring its own way (a hash) at `YACHIE_RING_SPEED` 0.6 a plain bullet's speed, straight; it opens out from its middle to `YACHIE_RING_CIRCLES` 3 circle radii and closes in again every `YACHIE_BREATHE_BEATS` 8, as a cosine (new `StageBullet.OrbitBreathe`); every arrowhead's body turns round once every `YACHIE_SPIN_BEATS` 2, each ring one way or the other. Read "arrowhead body turns" as each arrowhead spinning; the ring turning round its middle is the other reading. A ring starts from her middle, opening out first.
      - One lattice of cubes after another (the user's picture: a rhombille tiling, a vertical edge in every cube), edges `YACHIE_CUBE_CIRCLES` 4 circle radii long, over the whole screen, standing where she was: see-through and harmless for `YACHIE_PREVIEW_BEATS` 4 (`YACHIE_PREVIEW_ALPHA` 0.3), solid from its middle out over `YACHIE_ON_BEATS` 8 (Byakuren's beam sound), gone again from its middle out over `YACHIE_OFF_BEATS` 8, as Mai's snowflake; the fronts soft over `YACHIE_FRONT_CIRCLES` 1.5 circle radii. One seamless piece; where solid it hurts like a slider body.
    - **Rikako Asakura** (new `BossMechanic.Space`, `SpaceAct`, `Bullets/Asteroids.cs`; a placeholder until now): the user's text about the Earth, the asteroids and the satellite (`request.txt`).
      - The Earth (`Resources/Textures/rikako_earth.jpg`, the user's picture; `.jpg` resources are embedded now) over the whole screen in her attacks, `RIKAKO_EARTH_ALPHA` 0.5, under the bullets, fading in and out over a beat, with the sound of Yuyuko's tree growing as it comes.
      - The asteroids: 14 rocks cut out of the user's picture (new `Tools\Bake rocks`: the 16 biggest pieces, less a cluster of crumbs and one with crumbs stuck to it; `rikako_rock_N.png`, `Asteroids.HIT_SHARES` their hit circles). One every `RIKAKO_ROCK_EVERY_BEATS` 2 from `RIKAKO_ROCK_FILL_BEATS` 16 before her section (the screen is full of them as it begins; they fade in), each from any side through a place near the middle, `RIKAKO_ROCK_LEAST_CIRCLES`..`MOST` 2.5-6 circle radii, `RIKAKO_ROCK_SPEED_LEAST`..`MOST` 0.75-2 circle radii a beat, turning up to `RIKAKO_ROCK_SPIN_DEGREES` 12 a beat - all by a hash, so a seek and the autoplay see the same. Every bullet (not the beams) that flies into one on the screen bounces off it, `RIKAKO_ROCK_BOUNCES` 4 times at most, keeping its speed, with the ricochet flash (new `TrajectoryOpKind.Rocks`; what is pulled or falls flies through). They do not hurt (hurting like a slider body is the other reading). (Rings that the op is given live as long again, not 8 s more: `BulletEmitter.ConfigureOps`.)
      - A satellite (`rikako_satellite.png`, `RIKAKO_SATELLITE_CIRCLES` 7 circle radii across) as each attack begins, from out of the screen behind her (on the line from the player through her), flying that way at `RIKAKO_SATELLITE_SPEED` 1 circle radius a beat until it has left the screen, fading in over two beats; it faces the player, and every `RIKAKO_TURN_EVERY_BEATS` 4 turns (over as long) to face them again. Read "from Rikako's behind out of playfield" so; coming out from behind her picture is the other reading. Every `RIKAKO_BEAM_EVERY_BEATS` 16 a big beam both ways (`RIKAKO_BEAM_CIRCLES` 1.5 to each side), a see-through preview `RIKAKO_BEAM_PREVIEW_BEATS` 4, solid `RIKAKO_BEAM_ON_BEATS` 8 with the Master Spark's sound and a shake (`RIKAKO_SHAKE` 0.5).
      - Its square grid of beams (`RIKAKO_GRID_CIRCLES` 4 apart, `RIKAKO_GRID_ALPHA` 0.15 see-through), moved and turned with it; every `RIKAKO_WAVE_EVERY_BEATS` 8 a grey wave (`RIKAKO_WAVE_CIRCLES` 8 thick, `RIKAKO_WAVE_ALPHA` 0.3) runs out to past the farthest corner in `RIKAKO_WAVE_BEATS` 8 with Byakuren's beam sound, and the grid is solid under it, softly at its edges. The solid beam and grid hurt like a slider body.
    - All three in `own_spinner_bullets` (Yuiman's spinner stars round ones, out of her points); Rikako keeps the placeholder's spirals. Stars: Yuiman 3, Yachie 4, Rikako 4. Descriptions. `BossPeriod.CircleRadius` (the map's, for the ops).
    - Bench, Boss Rush 11:20 + 20 s, Always Boss, the song, autoplay (`AUTO_BUDGET=100`), 0 hits each: Yuiman 18.5 ms a frame (2.4 standing still; Shinki 13.6 / 2.9 the same way), Yachie 4.8-8.1 (1.8), Rikako 5.9 (3.9), Ariya 12.5 (7.5), Kogasa 3.3, Sara 2.7, Tewi 2.1, Orange 2.2, Rikako + Yuiman 23.2, Yachie + Rikako 7.6 (2.9).
574. **(2026-11-26) A round of 40: Iku reworked into stars, Noroiko's curses as the picture of 呪, Narumi's waves take everything, Cirno's shards for everything she freezes, central bosses shoot from the middle at once, and many tunings.** The user's list, with eight pictures (saved with the list in `Screenshots\2026-11-26 round 574`).
    - **Boss picker.** The most bosses is never more than the roster has (150, in whole sections of two or three: `BossPicker.setMostOnMap`), for Boss, Double and Triple Selection alike; it still starts at the most. The order numbers on the chosen heads are bold, yellow, on a dark badge with a blue rim (were small, with a shadow). The settings panel over the Danmakusu column is 1100 wide (760).
    - **Sara**: a ring every beat (`SARA_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 2 → 1).
    - **Nue**: for `NUE_PREVIEW_BEATS` 2 before her UFOs are sent see-through ones (`NUE_PREVIEW_ALPHA` 0.35) stand where they will land, with faint cones, until the real ones land on them.
    - **Yuiman Asama**: in her attacks each of her four points lets out a pair of her own bullets every `YUIMAN_OWN_EVERY_BEATS` 0.25, the way it fires, in its colour (read "she should shoot bullets from four points in arc streams" as her own streams; until then only what others fired came out of them); her arrowheads are `YUIMAN_ARROW_SIZE` 2 times as big, glow (`YUIMAN_ARROW_BLOOM` 1) and have a soft white halo (`YUIMAN_ARROW_HALO` 3).
    - **Noroiko**: a curse is the picture of 呪 now (the user's picture, baked by the new `Tools\Bake glyph` into `Resources/Textures/noroiko_curse.png` and `Bullets/CurseMask.cs`, its ink in 48 x 48 cells), white, over a soft white glow and a wider bloom (`NOROIKO_GLOW_*`, `NOROIKO_BLOOM_*`); its ink hurts like a slider body (it was drawn in thick red beams, which the user never saw as the character). A slider's curse is as big as the slider is long in time: `NOROIKO_SLIDER_CIRCLES_PER_BEAT` 4 circle radii a beat, 3..12 (`NOROIKO_SLIDER_MOST` 10 → 12; it was as big as the slider was wide).
    - **Ubame**: what her dolls shoot again is 3 times as big (`UBAME_RESHOT_SIZE` 1.5 → 3).
    - **Suika**: branches longer (`SUIKA_BRANCH_CIRCLES` 4.5 → 7), every one with two twigs and one near its root, the longer ones a fourth near the tip; the impact frame's white area has a soft edge 60 px wide (`IMPACT_BAND_FEATHER_PX` 14 → 60: Sumireko's band too).
    - **Sumireko**: her preview is as thick as the beam and half as solid as Matenshi's (`SUMIREKO_PREVIEW_ALPHA`), the impact frame flashes over the 840 ms right before the beam turns solid too, and it explodes into 192 stars (`SUMIREKO_STARS` 64), violet as the beam.
    - **Ariya**: her grids fly at half the speed (`ARIYA_SPEED` 0.8 → 0.4), a row every two beats (`ARIYA_ROW_EVERY_BEATS` 1 → 2, so that the rows stay as far apart).
    - **Nitori**: the sound of Yuyuko's petals flying off as her icosahedron comes (at the start of every attack, `BulletField.Cues`).
    - **Cirno** ("not all frozen bullets explode to shards"): what her freeze stopped and took away but did not burst were the rings fired more than 15 s before it (left out of the bursts), the rings made out of others, and the acts' bullets (frozen all, as they are not bullets of a ring). Now every ring that still lives bursts (only her own shards are not frozen), and the acts' bullets burst in the new `ShardsAct` as a ring's do (three shards, as big, as fast, round the way each flew).
    - **Orange**: everything turns round the middle once in `ORANGE_SPIN_BEATS` 32, clockwise - read "Orange spins" as the orange now turning (it did not turn); a flight out and back takes `ORANGE_FLIGHT_BEATS` 32 (16); for two beats before a slice comes back in over the edge a red arrow just inside the edge points in where it will come.
    - **Okina** ("I still see stretching beams"): not reproduced. The picture shows one straight beam from the portal on the left wall to the one on the right; the bench (the 11:32 map, Touhou EX Boss Rush Zenpen [Collab Marathon], Okina + Rumia at 8:36-8:50, with and without Always Boss, moving player, `PORTAL_LENGTH=1`, and `LONG_SHARE=0.25`, new: the share of the screen a piece must cross to count as long) shows no piece of a beam longer than a quarter of the screen and none stretched through the portals. Asked the user which map and settings.
    - **Yuyuko**: a bullet that leaves the bottom grows a flower only in her attacks (read "flower gathering is forever enabled" so: since 2026-11-01 one that left in a pause counted for the next attack).
    - **Merlin**: a safe place stays `MERLIN_SAFE_LINGER_BEATS` 4 after its circle is hit (its slider over, its follow points gone); the bullets in one are see-through too (`MERLIN_SAFE_SEE` 0.75, new `StageDim.See`) as well as dark, and her rings are drawn see-through and darker over one, in short arcs, softly to its edge (`TrumpetAct.ringOver`, `MERLIN_RING_DARK` 0.6; dark green arcs were laid over them), not darker over the circle or slider itself; all of it fades in and out with the place.
    - **Kana**: no sound as her bullets stop (Sakuya's time stop ticked).
    - **Shinki**: her streams turn half as fast (`SHINKI_STREAM_TURN_BEATS` 8 → 16), every bullet comes out four times (`SHINKI_BULLET_COPIES` 2 → 4).
    - **Mima**: her moon's body shoots its stars as soon as it starts to fade (it did once faded).
    - **Meira**: each of her three slides goes straight into where the player was as it starts, all the way, at half the speed (`MEIRA_SLIDE_SPEED` 6 → 3, as long as until the next one, `MEIRA_SLIDE_LONGEST_BEATS` 2 → 8; `MEIRA_SLIDE_SHARE` dropped: she went half the way to where Elly would stop, after the player); her beam half as thick (`MEIRA_TRACE_WIDTH` 1 → 0.5).
    - **Yachie**: the cubes' preview half as solid (`YACHIE_PREVIEW_ALPHA` 0.3 → 0.15), 24 arrowheads a ring (`YACHIE_RING_ARROWS` 12; read "rings density doubled" as twice as many in a ring - twice as many rings is the other reading).
    - **Rikako**: asteroids half the size and half as fast (`RIKAKO_ROCK_LEAST/MOST_CIRCLES` 2.5/6 → 1.25/3, `RIKAKO_ROCK_SPEED_LEAST/MOST` 0.75/2 → 0.375/1; `RIKAKO_ROCK_FILL_BEATS` 16 → 32). The satellite is drawn over its beam (in `stageFront`, over the bullets too); the beam is narrow where it leaves the satellite and widens as Marisa's spark does (`RIKAKO_BEAM_ROOT` 0.16 of its width, to all of it over `RIKAKO_BEAM_GROW_CIRCLES` 8), hurting as wide as it is drawn; it turns to the way it was last given evenly at `RIKAKO_TURN_DEGREES_PER_BEAT` 11.25 (read "its spin speed halved and constant" as the satellite's turn: it eased over four beats, a quarter turn at 22.5 a beat on average).
    - **Youmu**: 13 slashes at a time (`YOUMU_MORE_SLASHES` 17 → 12; "30% less").
    - **Narumi** ("waves are not working at all. Let her affect all bullets that already were spawned too"): her waves pushed only the rings fired in her section that could be pushed, and none that a boss's op acts on can be - with other bosses on the map nearly none. A wave now takes every ring's bullet it goes over (fired before her section too) and it flies on as her act's bullet, the way it went, `NARUMI_BOOST` times as fast (`StatueAct.Takes`; `Pushes` is gone).
    - **Takane**: her grabbing works on the bench (alone and with Cirno or Seija: 240 grabbed in the first 6 s of 11:35); fired before her section is grabbed too. Her big balls were not seen disappearing early there (none of them gone within 30 s, they live 29 s). Asked the user what they saw.
    - **Megumu**: every second bullet of a ring turns into a star (`EveryOther` on her freeze and bounce).
    - **Doremy**: the five dots come out of the very places they sat in - a slider's from its head, as its head is hit and they go (they came out of its end) - and as big as they were drawn.
    - **Nina**: her big balls every four beats (`NINA_BALL_EVERY_BEATS` 2 → 4).
    - **Iku reworked** (`BossMechanic.Thunder`): every ring of her section - the circles', the sliders', hers (now five bullets every `IKU_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 2, straight out; they curled) - is `IKU_STAR_POINTS` 5 bullets joined by straight gold beams as a five-pointed star that grows as they fly out (`BulletEmitter.StarLinked`, the planner at the end of Plan; `ThunderAct` draws and hurts). In every second stretch of `IKU_SWAP_BEATS` 4 of her section the beams of half the stars hurt like a slider body and those of the other half are see-through (`IKU_HARMLESS_ALPHA` 0.3) and harmless, then the other way round (`StarPhase`), fading over a sixth of a beat. The traces behind her bullets and the ties in fives are gone.
    - **Benben**: notes white (`BENBEN_NOTE_LIGHTER` 0.75 → 1), half the bloom (`BENBEN_NOTE_BLOOM` 1 → 0.5).
    - **Son Biten**: a slider's beam that began before her section and is still being fired as it begins spins too, from when it has all been fired (it was neither held nor spun).
    - **Tewi**: her own rings are ellipses, twice as often (`TEWI_OWN_INTERVAL` 0.5).
    - **Kanako**: everything a boss shoots in her section is red until it turns round and blue after (`BossOps.KanakoRed`/`KanakoBlue` on the turn's `During`/`After`; the beams keep dark blue, their turn an op of its own, `BulletField.beamOpsKept`); her talismans the same.
    - **Central bosses** ("perform their first initial attack out of centre, before their sections begin. Just like Kanako"): as a boss who stands in the middle takes her place from the boss before her, she glides there, and her first shots came from where that one stood (Kanako's first volley from the top of the screen, `KANAKO_PROBE`). A boss who stands in the middle (a spinner's, or one whose mechanic keeps her there) now shoots from where she goes (`BulletField.shotPlaceOf`: the rings, the beams, `bossPositionOf(period)` for the acts).
    - **Matenshi**: her feathers fly away from a place `MATENSHI_FEATHER_PIVOT_SCREEN` 0.25 of the screen below her, so that they go up over her too (from her middle nothing went up between her wings).
    - **Yoshika**: a returning talisman lives `YOSHIKA_RETURN_LIFE_BEATS` 4 (until it left the screen), and leaves its echoes again, as big as it is, standing end to end behind it, eight beats each (`NecromancyAct.leaveEchoes`; the train that followed it since 2026-11-22 is gone).
    - **Eiki**: for `EIKI_WARN_BEATS` 2 before her circles come, `EIKI_WARN_ARROWS` 8 big red arrows (`EIKI_WARN_ARROW_SIZE` 3 circle radii) stand on the edge of where each will be - round the player and round where they are mirrored - pointing in, blinking faster.
    - Descriptions and difficulty notes.
    - **Shinki and Yuiman (found on the bench; the user asked "Shinki is heavy?")**: an act keeps every bullet it made, and holds 60,000 at most: with four copies of every bullet Shinki's act was full after about 90 s of one section, and nothing more came out of her wings (on the bench at 11:20 of her Always Boss section she drew 48 things a frame, 937 the build before; two copies only put it off). Her wings' bullets and Yuiman's points' go into a pool of four acts now, 20 s of start times to each in turn, an act emptied when it gets a later stretch (`UI/Stages/ShotPool.cs`). A traced act bullet is surely gone once past its life and the freezes that can reach it (`BulletField.freezeReach`, by a binary search over the freezes; every freeze of the map was counted, minutes with Cirno all through a long section). Meira: the autoplay sees where a slide goes as it starts (5 hits, 0).
