Still heavy: Cirno + Shinki, 124 ms a frame standing still with the pair in every section (71 the build before, which did not show half of her streams; 214 with Always Boss) - every act bullet is traced through every op of the map, and with Cirno's freezes in every section that is hundreds; a lookup of the ops by time is the next step.

575. **(2026-11-27) The six of Highly Responsive to Prayers: Shingyoku, YuugenMagan, Elis, Sariel, Kikuri and Konngara (156 bosses), and Rikako tuned.** The user's list with their pictures (saved with the list in `Screenshots\2026-11-27 round 575`).
    - **Pictures.** The art of the six went into `old builds and assets\Touhou Characters`: Shingyoku, YuugenMagan, Elis and Kikuri cut out of their white backgrounds by `Bake cutout`, the white inside Elis' and YuugenMagan's outlines cleared by the new `Tools\Bake holes` (white regions flood-filled from seeds, with a soft rim; with no seeds it lists the white regions of 300 px or more to pick them from); Sariel and Konngara were transparent already. Sprites, heads and portraits made as SKILLS.md says; the ability sheet rows by `bosslist addrows`.
    - **Roster.** Six new entries in `BossRoster` with their colours and new mechanics `YinYang`, `EvilEyes`, `BigBat`, `AngelOfDeath`, `Underworld`, `Sweep`; all in `own_spinner_bullets` (no beams in their spinners, their own bullets for the stars); descriptions and stars (`BossDifficulty`: Shingyoku 3, YuugenMagan 4, Elis 3, Sariel 4, Kikuri 4, Konngara 3).
    - **Shingyoku** (new `UI/Stages/YinYangAct.cs`): every `SHINGYOKU_COOLDOWN_BEATS` 8 after her last throw is back she turns into a yin-yang orb `SHINGYOKU_ORB_SIZE` 1 x her magic circle, thrown at the player at `SHINGYOKU_ORB_SPEED` 1.5 x Reimu's orbs' speed, ricochets `SHINGYOKU_BOUNCES` 2 times off the edges of the screen and flies back to where it was thrown from; a red glow `SHINGYOKU_WARN_BEATS` 1 before. The orb hurts like a slider body; bullets that fly into it are put out on its edge and ricochet off it (`Pushes`). With other bosses on the screen she throws them instead, all at once (`SHINGYOKU_THROW_SPREAD_DEGREES` 20 apart), and they go on with their mechanics; one whose own act is moving her is not thrown, and her own act wins in the flight. New in the field: `StageAct.Carries` (an act places another boss; asked after the boss's own acts, `stageCarries`) and `stageHideRank` (an act hides the boss of one rank alone).
    - **YuugenMagan** (new `UI/Stages/EvilEyesAct.cs`): stands where Kaguya stands, with five eyes (`YUUGENMAGAN_EYE_CIRCLES` 1.5) on a pentagon whose top corner is she (`YUUGENMAGAN_PENTAGON` 1.3), joined by flickering see-through lightning that does not hurt. Every `YUUGENMAGAN_EVERY` 2nd bullet fired in her section is taken and shot from one of the four lower eyes at the player, basic, arrowhead, ellipse, star by eye. Her sliders' beams are Junko's, one beam each (`addLaser(..., single)`), from her (the top eye). Every `YUUGENMAGAN_THUNDER_BEATS` 16 every eye flashes white `YUUGENMAGAN_WARN_BEATS` 2, then strikes a bolt of Tojiko's lightning (`StormAct.zigzag`/`jagged`, now internal) a way of its own, twice as thick, twice as many branches twice as long, solid `YUUGENMAGAN_BOLT_BEATS` 2. Circles hit and sliders over are thrown from her eyes in turn at the player, golden (`ObjectFallAct.FromEye`, `YUUGENMAGAN_GOLD`).
    - **Elis** (new `UI/Stages/BigBatAct.cs`): every `ELIS_EVERY_BEATS` 24 a red flash `ELIS_WARN_BEATS` 2, then a big bat (`ELIS_BAT_CIRCLES` 2.4, drawn red; the boss is hidden) that chases the player `ELIS_FLY_BEATS` 8 at Koakuma's bats' speed and turning (`BatAct.steer`, now internal), hurting (`ELIS_HIT_CIRCLES` 1.1) and taking the bullets within `ELIS_RECORD_CIRCLES` 2.4 (`ELIS_RECORD_MOST` 128); then she stops and over `ELIS_SHOOT_BEATS` 8 shoots them back evenly, all the way from her to the player as she stopped, spread across her magic circle, `ELIS_SHOT_SPEED` 2 x as fast and `ELIS_SHOT_SIZE` 2 x as big, red, each with a beam behind it `ELIS_TRACE_BEATS` 4 long (`ELIS_TRACE_WIDTH` 0.3) that hurts.
    - **Sariel** (new `UI/Stages/AngelOfDeathAct.cs`): in her attacks a ring of `SARIEL_RING_BATS` 10 bats every `SARIEL_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 8, Koakuma's at `SARIEL_BAT_SLOW` 0.5 speed and homing, gone after `SARIEL_BAT_LIFE_BEATS` 16; every beam of her section whose end leaves the screen lets a full Koakuma bat in there (`VampireAct.leaves`, now internal; `SARIEL_BATS_MOST` 48 at once); every bat shoots a basic bullet at the player every `SARIEL_STREAM_BEATS` 1. Every `SARIEL_WRAP_EVERY` 8th bullet that leaves by the bottom comes in at the top half as big, `SARIEL_WRAPS` 3 times (top to bottom too). After her last section on the map (`BossOps.IsLastOfHers`): everything turns dark purple (a `Turn` by nothing with `BossOps.SarielPurple`, new `BulletRenderer.TINT_DARK_PURPLE` 18, `max_styles` 40 → 41), the screen dims (new `sarielBackground`, `SARIEL_DIM` 0.6 fading over `SARIEL_DIM_BEATS` 8) and `SARIEL_WARN_BEATS` 2 later everything, bullets and beams, turns at the player (an `Aim` with `PlayerAim` her act places), red arrows beside every bullet before it.
    - **Kikuri** (new `UI/Stages/UnderworldAct.cs`): always in the middle (`bossAtCentre`); as each attack begins everything, bullets and beams, turns to fly straight out from the middle, and what is fired in it too (new `TrajectoryOpKind.FaceOut`, `BossOps`; a window op on both lists), warned by `KIKURI_WARN_ARROWS` 8 big red arrows round her `KIKURI_WARN_BEATS` 2 before; every `KIKURI_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 4 of an attack a ring of `KIKURI_RING_BEAMS` 36 of Shou's waving beams; every `KIKURI_RESHOOT_EVERY` 4th bullet that leaves the screen is shot again from her at the player.
    - **Konngara** (new `UI/Stages/SweepAct.cs`, a `CuttingAct`): every `KONNGARA_EVERY_BEATS` 16 an endless beam as thin as a slider (`KONNGARA_BEAM_WIDTH` 1) from her straight up, a preview `KONNGARA_PREVIEW_BEATS` 4, then one sweep round her in `KONNGARA_SPIN_BEATS` 4 with Byakuren's beam sound (a cue), fading `KONNGARA_FADE_BEATS` 0.5; bullets in its way are cut in two, the halves flying apart square to it. Its hit covers what it went over in the last 24 ms (it turns many times its width a frame far from her: standing still it was never hit).
    - **Rikako**: her satellite turns half as fast (`RIKAKO_TURN_DEGREES_PER_BEAT` 11.25 → 5.625); twice as many asteroids, half as big (`RIKAKO_ROCK_EVERY_BEATS` 2 → 1, `RIKAKO_ROCK_LEAST_CIRCLES` 1.25 → 0.625, `RIKAKO_ROCK_MOST_CIRCLES` 3 → 1.5); when the satellite starts turning the other way than it last did its grid flashes (`RIKAKO_TURN_FLASH_BEATS` 0.5). (The bench: her collisions cost 0.9 → 1.8 ms a frame, twice the rocks to look at.)
    - **Bench** (Boss Rush Lunatic, 2:00 + 30 s, Always Boss, the autoplay): Shingyoku 2.2 ms a frame, YuugenMagan 2.5, Elis 3.4, Sariel 3.0, Kikuri 5.7, Konngara 7.4 (4.8 standing still), Rikako 5.0; no autoplay hits; pairs and Shingyoku + Elis + Sariel 2-5 ms.

577. **(2026-11-27) Six columns of bosses, and the Marathon mod.** The user's request with a screenshot of the boss picker (saved in `Screenshots\2026-11-27 round 577`).
    - **Boss picker**: six heads in a row (`BossPicker.PickerControl.columns` 4 → 6).
    - **Marathon** (new `MR`, `Mods/Marathon.cs` + `Mods/MarathonPicker.cs`, in the Danmakusu column): several maps played back to back. Its setting is a list of the library's maps with a search box that is song select's (osu!'s `FilterQueryParser.ApplyQueries`, by reflection, and `BeatmapCarouselFilterMatching.CheckCriteriaMatch`, for Danmakusu: converted maps, Danmakusu stars); a click checks a map (its box shows its number in the order) or takes it out; the checked ones are listed first, then the first 200 found; Clear takes them all out. The value is the maps' MD5s in order. Starting any map in song select starts the first one; a map played to its end (`Player.OnShowingResults`, not a replay; the autoplay counts) makes the next one due: its results show "Marathon: map n of m in 5 s - ..." and leave by themselves after `MarathonRun.NEXT_SECONDS` 5 (or when left), and song select, coming back, puts the next map on and starts it (Harmony: `SoloSongSelect.OnStart` prefix, `SoloSongSelect.OnResuming` postfix, `ResultsScreen.OnEntering` postfix). Quitting or failing a map ends the marathon; a map no longer in the library is left out.
    - **Tools**: `refcheck types <folder> <part> ...` and `refcheck members <folder> <Type> [part]` list what lazer's assemblies have (to find the way into its screens); `sectioncheck marathon [queries]` checks Marathon's patches and its search on eight made-up maps.

578. **(2026-11-27) Marathon: the bosses of the whole marathon, and no pauses between maps.** The user: "marathon should affect max boss count too", "also no pauses between maps".
    - **Boss count**: with Marathon on (with maps), the boss picker's most is what all its maps have room for together (each map read in the background once for every attacks-per-boss and Always Boss setting, `BossPicker.BarPreview.roomOf`; the label says "up to N in the marathon's M maps"), and the bosses are spread over the maps in order: one section for every map first, the rest by how much more room each has (`BossPicker.SplitShares`). The shares are kept in the boss selection value ("count;attacks;share.share|ids", so a score keeps them; values without them read as before), and each map of the marathon plays its share (`IBossSelection.Choose(fromColours, md5)`, `MarathonRun.IndexOf`; shares that do not fit the marathon: an even split). The bar preview shows the selected map's share.
    - **No pauses**: the results of a map with another after it are left at once (no five-second line: `NEXT_SECONDS` gone), the next map is put on and started on the next frame (250 ms before), and its loader is osu!'s quick restart (`PlayerLoader.QuickRestart`, set by reflection: without it only as slow as a loader is).
    - `sectioncheck marathon` also prints the split for a few counts and rooms and reads the values back.

579. **(2026-11-27) Marathon: seamless, one boss bar, Random and the modes.** The user: "Switch between marathon maps - seamless. Random button left to clear. Also field for random maps amount. Default 5. Random chooses among found maps. Also 4 checks for main mods filter. Default standard only checked", then "also one boss bar shared among all maps".
    - **Seamless**: when a map with another after it is over, a black curtain comes down over the whole game at once (added to osu!'s game by song select's `game`), osu!'s second before the results is cut (`Player.progressToResults` prefix: no delay for a marathon's player, registered by `ApplyToPlayer`; not a replay), the results are left at once, the next map starts on the next frame with a quick-restart loader, and the curtain goes up as its player comes in (`Player.OnEntering` postfix); it goes up by itself after 15 s, and when the marathon ends.
    - **One boss bar** (new `Mods/MarathonBar.cs`): as a marathon starts, its other maps are read in the background (`BulletPlanner.BarPeriods` with their share of the bosses, their colours, how long they are); each map's field puts its own sections among them on one line - the maps before it empty, the ones after it full, every map's boss slots after the ones before (`BulletField.ShareMarathonBar`, the HUD now in a slot of its own, `hudSlot`). The first map waits for the reading (30 s at most); without a boss selection mod each map keeps its own bar.
    - **Picker**: four mode checks under the search (osu!, taiko, catch, mania; osu!standard alone at first) - only maps of the checked modes are found; "Random maps" (5 at first) and Random, left of Clear: that many maps at random among everything found (not only the 200 shown), replacing what was checked. Both are kept while the game runs.

580. **(2026-11-27) Marathon starts with the selected map.** The user: "Marathon - choosen map initially choosen as default". When Marathon is first put on with nothing checked, the map selected in song select is checked as its first map (`MarathonPicker`, once for every Marathon setting: a list cleared later stays clear).

581. **(2026-11-27) Marathon without the seamless switch, no boss twice, one bar; Lunasa and Kutaka built; fourteen bosses tuned.** The user's list.
   - **Marathon.** "rollback seamless switching": the black curtain between maps, `Player.progressToResults` without its delay and the patch on the player's entering are gone. Between maps the game goes through results (left at once) and song select (the next map started from there, a quick restart) as osu! does itself. "same bosses appearing again even if max amount of bosses are chosen": osu!'s log showed the marathon going A B C A B C D E. After the third map song select put back a map its filter hid and the start was taken for a new marathon. Now a marathon that is starting its next map (`MarathonRun.starting`) is never started again, and song select's own checks of the selected map are skipped meanwhile (`ensureGlobalBeatmapValid`, `checkBeatmapValidForSelection`). The bosses filled in by colour were chosen per map, so they came again too. Now the whole marathon's bosses are chosen once (`BossPicker.MarathonBosses`: the picked ones, then distinct ones, the modified first, in an order seeded by the maps), and every map takes its own share of that list in turn (`Choose(fromColours, md5)`). The immortals still come again, as intended. "Boss bar is still its own for each map": the bar's maps are now read one after another on the update thread from the beatmap manager's own query (`MarathonBar.Prepare`), and osu!'s log says how many were read and which bar every map keeps or shows ("Marathon bar: k of n maps read", "Marathon map i of n: ..."), to find what is wrong if it is still one bar per map.

She herself shoots 5 parallel straight long laserbeams towards player with preview. Duration 4 beats, every 8 beats. While laserbeam is active - all osu objects and sliders instead of scattering to pieces - get slashed after finishing instead." Every 8 beats (`LUNASA_EVERY_BEATS`) she fires 5 long straight beams side by side, 2.5 circle radii apart (`LUNASA_BEAMS`, `LUNASA_BEAM_GAP_CIRCLES`), at the player: a see-through preview for 2 beats, then solid for 4 (`LUNASA_PREVIEW_BEATS`, `LUNASA_BEAM_BEATS`), hurting like a slider body. Every circle of her section breaks as it is hit into 6 pie pieces (`LUNASA_PIECES`), and every slider as it is over into a piece every two circle radii along it. The pieces are drawn in the object's colour with no glow, fly out at a bullet's speed (`LUNASA_PIECE_SPEED` 1) and hurt like a slider body. While her beams are solid an object is cut in two along them instead, and the halves fly apart across them (a `CuttingAct`). Her spinner shoots her own bullets (no beams). Description and 4 stars.
   - **Kutaka Niwatari (new, `Rooster`, `UI/Stages/StreamsAct.cs`).** "all bullet speed halved, but osu objects and sliders shoot out all their bullets initially aimed toward players in streams of two instead". Every ring of her section flies at half speed and lives twice as long (`KUTAKA_SLOW` 0.5, in the planner). What the circles and sliders fire is taken as it is fired and shot again from where it was fired at the player, in two streams one circle radius apart (`KUTAKA_STREAM_APART_CIRCLES`), the ring's bullets two at a time, a pair every eighth of a beat (`KUTAKA_STREAM_GAP_BEATS`), at their own (halved) speed. What bosses shoot is left alone. Her spinner shoots her own bullets. Description and 3 stars.

582. **(2026-11-28) Sumireko's frame see-through; Marathon carries the bullets on the screen to the next map.** The user.
   - **Sumireko.** "transparency doubled means double the transparency": the impact frame before her beam turns solid inverts at a quarter (`SUMIREKO_IMPACT_BEFORE_STRENGTH` 0.5 → 0.25). Half turned inside out is an even grey, and nothing showed through it. At a quarter the field shows through at half its contrast: three quarters see-through.
   - **Marathon.** "transfer remaining bullets on the screen to next map": when a map of a marathon is over (its results are about to be shown), every bullet still on the screen is taken as it is (`BulletField.CarryOff`, new `UI/BulletField.Carry.cs`): the rings' and the acts' bullets, where each is, the way and speed it flies (from where it was 16 ms before; what stands still or has just jumped flies as its ring does), its size, shape, tint and colour. At most 4000. The next map of the marathon starts with them (`CarryIn`, `CarryAct`): from its first frame they fly on straight from there as bullets of their own, hurt as any bullet does, and leave the screen. They take the colour of the new map nearest to their own. What stands still fades out after 3 s, and nothing stays longer than 20 s. When the intro is skipped they go on from where they had got to. Beams, what an act draws as a picture of its own, what cannot hurt and Yukari's trains stay behind. A map played again starts with them again. `MarathonRun.Joined` hands them over and keeps the field of the map played now. osu!'s log says how many went on ("Marathon: n bullets go on to the next map", "Marathon map i starts with n bullets of the map before"). The mod's description says so too.

Standing still the player is hit 3 times by them, the autoplay once. Sakuya + Utsuho at 3:10, then Lunasa: 825 carried (knives, circles, ellipses, stars), 10 hits standing still. The second map costs 0.5-0.9 ms a frame.

583. **(2026-11-28) Noroiko's curses at last, Narumi's waves seen; Lunasa and Momoyo reworked; Marathon carries beams; Seija's five mirrors; thirty bosses tuned.** The user's list (saved with its four pictures in `Screenshots\2026-11-28 round 583`).
   - **Noroiko** ("her bullets are still not replaced by jp letter shape laserbeams, and sliders don't shoot growing jp laserbeams too"). Two causes. First, `BulletField` built the stage acts before it set `circleRadius`, so every size `CurseAct` worked out in its constructor was 0: since 2026-11-23 every curse was a dot. The bench's hits were that dot homing into the player. The assignment now comes before `buildStage`; no other act reads it in a constructor. Second, in a section of two bosses (the user always plays two) every ring is halved after the mechanics are planned, so the 呪 rings fell apart into 20 scattered bullets. Cursed rings now keep their count (`KeepCount`; `Halved`/`Thirded` give the ring itself back for `CurseBeams`). New: every ring of the map in her section (rings with no layout too, `AsEvenRing`; not the bosses' own) is 呪 drawn in beams (`BulletEmitter.CurseBeams`, its bullets hidden, `HiddenMask` carried by `copy()`). It has 25 bullets, one on every corner (`RingLayout.GlyphCorners` / `GlyphBetween` / `GlyphRuns`; `glyph()` puts a bullet on every corner). The new `CurseRingsAct` (in `CurseAct.cs`) draws slider-wide beams in the ring's colours that hurt like a slider body, with `GridShapes` for the autoplay; the corners are worked out once a moment, in parallel (with Kokoro 31 → 8.7 ms a frame). Each glyph grows until its farthest corner is `NOROIKO_RING_REACH_CIRCLES` 6 circle radii out, fades over the last `NOROIKO_RING_FADE_SHARE` 0.25 and is harmless past half faded. Without the cap the glyphs walled off the screen: 34 autoplay hits in 20 s. The sliders' curses grow from `NOROIKO_SLIDER_GROW_FROM` 0.25 of their size to all of it over the homing. `NOROIKO_RING_LEAST` is gone. Stars 4 → 5.
   - **Narumi** ("waves still not working"). The bench showed the waves taking bullets (about 3,900 in 20 s with Yuki). But since 2026-11-25 ("wave speed buff 2x") a taken bullet flew at twice the speed it had: in her section that is a quarter of its own, so a half. Hardly seen. Now it flies at `NARUMI_BOOST` times its own full speed (`speed / NARUMI_SLOW` for the rings she slowed): 0.13 → 1.03 times a plain bullet, eight times faster. Her balls' rings too. Stars 3 → 4.
   - **Lunasa** ("laserbeams are slider laserbeams thick. Shoot out of 5 points on magic circle edge, keep the distance in-between laserbeams. Byakuren sound plays. All bullets touching laserbeams get slashed. Duration doubled. not parallel anymore, spread 30 angles. In gaps laserbeams of gap size come out as 5k osumania convert notes"):
     - Beams: as thick as a slider's (`LUNASA_BEAM_HALF_CIRCLES` 0.5 gone) and solid for `LUNASA_BEAM_BEATS` 4 → 8 beats, so the next volley's preview comes over the last two beats of this one. They start on the edge of her magic circle, `LUNASA_BEAM_GAP_CIRCLES` apart along it (at most `LUNASA_EDGE_MOST_DEGREES` 45° between two), and fan out over `LUNASA_SPREAD_DEGREES` 30° round the player. Byakuren's beam sound (`touhou_strike`) plays as each volley turns solid (`ViolinAct.SolidTimes`).
     - Cuts: every ring bullet that touches a solid beam, or crosses it, is cut in two along it (`Takes`, `CutBullet`). A circle or slider is cut along the nearest beam.
     - Notes: they come from an osu!mania convert with a key for every gap. Five beams leave four gaps, so it is four keys (planner `Violin` pass 0 → `BossPeriod.FallingNotes`; a separate convert from Yatsuhashi's). A note that comes while the beams are solid comes out of the edge of her circle between two beam starts on its time. It is a beam laid across the gap, as long as the gap is wide there; a held note is a band as long as it is held. It flies out along the gap, crossing a screen's height in `LUNASA_NOTE_BEATS` 4, drawn every `LUNASA_NOTE_STEP_PX` 24, on to the edge of the screen, and hurts like a slider body.
     - `Hurts` now looks only at the circles' and sliders' pieces (`sliderPieces`); the cut bullets' pieces hit as bullets do. With every bullet cut, walking all the pieces cost 6.3 ms a frame; now 0.4.

Centipede - high resolution, Her body, legs, scales move separately in joints, centipede can slowly turn into player and move away. Other Body parts move too. Also there is a untouched while bg in-between tails too"):
     - Blocks: a block no longer flies from her. It slides out of the wall next to its cell (the inner ring out of the block in front of it, drawn under it) over `MOMOYO_THROW_BEATS` 0.5 → 2. One still comes every half beat.
     - Centipede: the user's picture was 400 x 211, too small to be sharp, with white left between its tails. It is now put together of pieces drawn in the picture's colours (new `StageTexture.CentipedePlate`, `CentipedeHead`, `CentipedeLeg`, 256 px; `momoyo_centipede.png` removed). It has `MOMOYO_CENTIPEDE_PLATES` 22 plates, each turned along its body, following its head's path. The path sways `MOMOYO_SWAY` 0.3 of its width every `MOMOYO_SWAY_LENGTH` 0.6 of its length.
     - Legs and feelers: a pair of legs on every plate, each of two joints (`MOMOYO_LEG_UPPER` 0.55, `MOMOYO_LEG_LOWER` 0.7 times its width). They stroke `MOMOYO_LEG_STROKE_DEGREES` 22 once every `MOMOYO_LEG_STRIDE_CIRCLES` 1.5 circle radii it goes, a wave running `MOMOYO_LEG_WAVES` 1.5 times down its body. The plates rock, the fangs open and close, and the feelers at its head and its tail wave.

   - **Marathon** ("carry beams to the next map too"): the lasers on the screen that are not over (and not all harmless) go on too, at most 20,000 points (`BulletField.CarryOffBeams`, `CarriedBeam`: their points, how fast each moves, half widths and colours; a turning beam's points straight out from its middle). `CarryIn(bullets, beams)`. `CarryAct` draws them and they hurt as beams do (`GridShapes` for the autoplay), until the slowest point is off the screen. osu!'s log says "n bullets and m beams"; so does the mod's description; `sectioncheck carry` prints the beams. Bench (Yuki + Benben 2:00 + 5 s, then Reimu): 405 bullets and 88 beams carried, 8 beam hits standing still.
   - **Seija** ("first attack is full mirror. Second attack is horizontal mirror (only horizontal arrows are warning), third attack is vertical mirror (only vertical arrows warning), fourth attack only playfield is mirrored, fifth attack - playfield stays as but player is mirrored"):
     - Kinds: `BulletField.MirrorKind` (`Full`, `Horizontal`, `Vertical`, `ViewOnly`, `PlayerOnly`) goes by her attack's number in the section, in turn (`MirrorKindOf`). `MirrorAngleAt` turns only for `Full`/`ViewOnly`. `MirrorFlipAt` folds the playfield over (a scale through 0 by a cosine, never under 0.02). `MirrorCursorAt` says where the character goes: with the view, under the mouse, or mirrored (`PlayerOnly`, at once for the attack). `MirrorAmountAt` and `MirrorKindAt` complete the set.
     - What follows the kind: the playfield's `Scale` (times the Focus Lens zoom), the HUD (counter-flipped, `BossHud.SetMirror(deg, centre, flip)`), the boss preview, `unview`, and a sweep off when the flip or the cursor's rule changes.
     - `InversionAct`: only sideways arrows for horizontal and only up-and-down ones for vertical; her clone is mirrored by the kind (none when only the player is); her glow goes by how far the mirror is.
   - **YuugenMagan** ("Eyes shoot parallel to line between boss and player, and also in 30 degree evenly spread too. This is her circles now. yuugen eye image here, simplify. Circle radius remains, eye is twice less radius, pupil always looking at player"):
     - Shots: an eye shoots a taken bullet parallel to the line from her to the player, and `YUUGENMAGAN_SPREAD` 1 more on each side at `YUUGENMAGAN_SPREAD_DEGREES` 30. That is three for every bullet; one was aimed from the eye before.
     - Eyes: the circles are a swirl (`Resources/Textures/yuugen_swirl.png`, cut from the user's picture by the new `Tools\Bake disc <in> <out> <size> [cx cy r]`). It turns (`YUUGENMAGAN_SWIRL_TURN_BEATS` 8, every second eye the other way) with a plain eye in it, half the radius, whose pupil looks at the player (`StageTexture.YuugenSwirl`, in the back layer when she is on the map).
     - Stars 4 → 5.
   - **Sanae** ("half of all bullets come out as star shaped rings from spinning big marisa star from the centre"): in her attacks every second ring (not the bosses' own) is `BulletEmitter.EvenRingFrom` (new): a star of twice as many bullets from the middle of the screen, turned as the star spins (`BulletPlanner.SanaeStarAngle`, `SANAE_SOURCE_DEGREES_PER_SECOND` 120), not aimed. `MiracleAct` draws the big Marisa star spinning there (`SANAE_SOURCE_CIRCLES` 2.5) with a glow. Stars 3 → 4.
   - **Tsukasa** ("shoots big marisa stars instead. Recorded bullets come out evenly in star shape every 4 beats instead (each star shoots separately)"): her big balls are soft (Marisa) stars. Every tenth bullet is kept by the next star, each star its own. Every `TSUKASA_RECORD_EVERY_BEATS` 4 each star lets out what it kept at once along a five-pointed star round it (`RingLayout` star, first point at the player, from its edge, at their average speed, growing as it flies). A star that is gone by then takes them with it.
   - **Meira** ("half the trace thickness. Trace is seamless. she doesn't run mid flying. Double the flying speed. She shoots talismans instead, fan of 5"): `MEIRA_TRACE_WIDTH` 0.5 → 0.25; a round's beams are one piece (seamless); `MEIRA_SLIDE_SPEED` 3 → 6. Read "doesn't run mid flying" as no standing between flights: `MEIRA_FLIGHTS` 3 one after the other, each starting as the one before arrives (`MEIRA_FLIGHT_LEAST_BEATS` 0.25 at least), then she stands; `MEIRA_SLIDES` / `MEIRA_SLIDE_LONGEST_BEATS` gone. Chained all through the 8 beats they were 25 a round and the autoplay was hit 15 times; with 3 it is hit 4 times. Her fans are talismans (`addMeiraFans`).
   - **Kisume** ("she shoots 4 beat long straight laserbeams down instead. Also rope connects her and top wall"): her three beams are the planner's lasers now (`WellBucket` pass 0, at `BossOps.KisumeStops`, shared with `BucketAct`). After `KISUME_PREVIEW_BEATS` 1 they fire for `KISUME_BEAM_BEATS` 4 from where she stands, straight, down and 15° to each side (`Fan` 1e-4 with `FanCentre`), as thick as a slider's beam, silent (her act plays the sound). `KISUME_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 8, `KISUME_STOP_BEATS` 2 → 5. The act draws the preview and a rope from the top of the screen to her (`KISUME_ROPE_CIRCLES` 0.1); its `Hurts` is gone.
   - **Iku** ("laserbeams do not disconnect by themselves"): what broke a star by itself was one of its corners leaving the screen. Now a star stays whole (its corners by the ring's closed form, used-up or taken ones too) while any corner is on the screen, and is gone at once when none is. Never breaking at all made the autoplay be hit 42 times in 20 s (the inner chords sweep the screen).
   - **Iwanaga** ("if frozen slider is sliderwhistle - shoot out laserbeams too"): every slider over in a stretch with a whistle (body or head) fires its beam along its path when the stretch ends (`addLaser` + `Later`), unless its beam already starts in the stretch.
   - **Rin** ("Double the amount of freezing bullets. 1.5x more size"): `RIN_FREEZE_AFTER_BEATS` 16 → 8 (read as twice as many swollen on the screen at once; alternative: rings fired before her section too), `RIN_SWELL` 3 → 4.5. Stars 4 → 5.
   - **Sumireko** ("harmless outlines of laserbeams even more transparent"): `SUMIREKO_PREVIEW_ALPHA` Matenshi's x 0.5 → x 0.25; the fade reaches all three layers of the beam.
   - **Konngara** ("spin speed halved. Laserbeam is white body"): `KONNGARA_SPIN_BEATS` 16 → 32, `KONNGARA_EVERY_BEATS` 28 → 44 (the next still 8 beats after); the beam's body white (pale pink before).
   - **Kanako** ("circles grow from centre instead"): her circles come out of her middle (`hubOut` 0), growing as they fly.
   - **Shinki**: her wings' bullets are white (`TINT_WHITE`).
   - **Yuuka** ("sunflower body and green petals deal dmg too"): the stems hurt like a slider body (`SunflowerAct.Hurts`), the green leaves as bullets; the yellow petals stay harmless. Stars 2 → 3.
   - **Sunny Milk** ("bullets that are in prism and about to disperse also leave trace in prism too"): bullets crossing the prism leave a tail in it too (`prismTails`, drawn only: the prism hurts anyway).
   - **Takane** ("double the orbit size, orbit edges are visible with circle outlines, green, transparent"): `TAKANE_ORBIT_SCALE` 2; every orbit is drawn as a tilted green ellipse (`StageLayer.AddEllipse`, new; `TAKANE_OUTLINE_WIDTH` 0.06 circle radii, `TAKANE_OUTLINE_ALPHA` 0.3).
   - **Marisa** ("outlines are harmful too"): her Master Sparks hurt over their whole drawn width, edges included (`BulletField.beamHitScale`: 1 for `Immune` beams; in the hits and the autoplay; the planner's `HitHalfWidth` full).
   - **Doremy** ("5 dot bullets size gradually change. 1x when on centre. 3x when on playfield edge"): `BulletEmitter.EdgeGrow` / `EdgeScale` (new), up to `DOREMY_DOT_EDGE_SIZE` 3 at the playfield's edge; drawn, hit, in the broad phase (`BigScaleMax`) and in the autoplay's reach.
   - **Sariel** ("all her bats have double the size"): `SARIEL_BAT_SIZE` 2 (Koakuma's bats her beams let in too).
   - **Ellen** ("connection duration doubled"): `ELLEN_BEAM_SOLID_BEATS` 2 → 4.
   - **Megumu** ("halve the star bullets amount"): every fourth bullet becomes a star (`MEGUMU_STARS_OF_EIGHT` 2, every second before).
   - **Yuyuko** ("flowers keep spawning on tree right after shooting away flowers too"): an attack's flowers begin to grow as soon as the last attack's have flown off, in the pause (`TreeAct.flowering`, `flowersFrom`); sowing stays in the attacks.
   - **Star Sapphire** ("after her section is done she shoots away last white star sphere"): one more white sphere thrown alone as her section ends, with the petals' sound; her act lasts until it has grown away.
   - **Aya** ("half thick straight laserbeams. Feathers half the size. Double the flying frequency. Double the previews duration"): `AYA_BEAM_WIDTH` 0.5, `AYA_FEATHER_SIZE` 0.5, `AYA_DASH_EVERY_BEATS` 0.5 → 0.25 with `AYA_REST_LEAST_BEATS` 0.125, `AYA_PREVIEW_BEATS` 2 (the ways chosen two beats ahead, previews chained; `AYA_PREVIEW_LEAST_BEATS` gone).
   - **Ringo** ("halve the dangos amount. Halve the triplets amount"): only every second ring of her attacks leaves copies (`RINGO_THREE_EVERY` 2); the skewers stay on every second three. Stars 3 → 2.
   - **Medicine** ("small pink wave flash on bullets clash zone"): a small pink wave (`MEDICINE_WAVE_MS` 350, growing to `MEDICINE_WAVE_GROW` 4 times) where two bullets met, instead of the white flash.
   - **Orange** ("arrow warnings are outside orange. Yuyuko tree flower leave sound plays. Orange grow and shoot frequency doubled"): `ORANGE_ORANGES` 2 → 4 taking turns; the arrows lie outside the rind; the petals' sound at every split (`CitrusAct.SplitTimes`). Stars 3 → 4.
   - **Aunn** ("chasing bullets speed halved"): `AUNN_MOVE_SPEED` 2 → 1. Stars 4 → 3.
   - **Suika** ("impact frame edges triple more smooth area. crack bloom absolute seamless, no overlaps"): `SUIKA_IMPACT_FEATHER_PX` 60 → 180 (Sumireko keeps 60); the cracks' glow pieces are `MeshQuad.Brightest` (new): the renderer draws them with Max blending, so crossing glows and round ends do not add up.
   - **Merlin** ("fix dim and bloot segmentation"): her rings are mesh pieces (`stageMeshes`, a black band and the body, `ring_step_px` 6, 24-512 a ring) instead of arcs: the arcs showed seams and their pool of 384 ran out.
   - **Youmu** ("30% less amount of laserbeams"): `YOUMU_MORE_SLASHES` 12 → 8 (13 → 9 slashes), `YOUMU_RAYS` 16 → 11.
   - **Rikako** ("satelite turn frequency halved. Turn speed halved. Movement speed halved. Spin direction warning flash - not laserbeams itselves, but white bloom around it"): `RIKAKO_TURN_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 8, `RIKAKO_TURN_DEGREES_PER_BEAT` 5.625 → 2.8125, `RIKAKO_SATELLITE_SPEED` 1 → 0.5; the flash is a white light round the grid lines (`LaserInstance.BloomUnfaded`, new: a bloom that ignores the beam's fades; `RIKAKO_FLASH_BLOOM` 1).

At the next edge each of those splits again into 5 plain bullets, which fly on and out.

588. **(2026-11-28) Marathon's Random leaves the checked maps alone.** The user: "Already Selected songs are not affected by random". 589. **(2026-11-28) Noroiko's curses half as big, Rikako's Earth all through her section, the selected map always a marathon's first, Suika's tails and fissure halved.** The user.
   - **Noroiko** ("halve her homing curses size"): all her curses that home - hers and the sliders' - are half as big, their strokes with them (the same strokes on a curse half as big closed it up). `NOROIKO_CURSE_CIRCLES` 4 → 2, `NOROIKO_STROKE_CIRCLES` 0.24 → 0.12, `NOROIKO_SLIDER_CIRCLES_PER_BEAT` 4 → 2, `NOROIKO_SLIDER_LEAST` 3 → 1.5, `NOROIKO_SLIDER_MOST` 12 → 6. Her 呪 rings are not curses that home: as they were.
   - **Rikako** ("background stays during her section"): the Earth seen from orbit lies over the screen all through her section, fading in as it begins and out as it ends; it came and went with each attack.
   - **Marathon** ("Selected song is locked and is first"): the map selected in song select is always the marathon's first. In the picker (`MarathonPicker.applyLock`) it is put first and marked "(selected, locked)". It cannot be taken out, Clear leaves it, Random adds after it, and when another map is selected that one takes its place. When a marathon starts (`MarathonRun.startPrefix`) the map started is put first in the list and in the setting, so the bosses' shares go by it, even if the picker was never opened. Until now the first checked map was started whichever map was selected, and the selected one was checked as the first only when Marathon was first put on with nothing checked. The mod's description says so.
   - **Suika** ("halve the traces length. Halve the spread out bullets count"): the tail behind what her stomp throws is a beat long (`SUIKA_TAIL_BEATS` 2 → 1). Read "the spread out bullets" as the glowing ellipses her big crack explodes into off both its sides: `SUIKA_FISSURE_BULLETS` 4 → 2 from each place to each side. (The other reading: the two ellipses a thrown bullet bursts into at the wall, `SUIKA_WALL_PIECES` 2 → 1.)
   - Descriptions. Bench (the Boss Rush 1:00 + 30 s, the autoplay): Rikako 0 hits, Suika 0. Noroiko 3, all from her 呪 rings - unchanged this round - from 1:19.9 on (1 in the 20 s benched in round 583).

A ring more than half faded no longer hurts, and the autoplay looked at how faded it was at the nearest moment of its grid. A little later, it was skipped while it still hurt. Now it counts down to 0.3 (`CurseRingsAct.auto_solid_least`): 4 hits (3 rings, 1 curse). Padding the strokes by each ring's fastest corner instead was tried first and changed nothing.

428. **(2026-11-12) Boss ability sheet: built bosses green, placeholders colourless.** The user. The bosslist tool's third argument now colours a built boss's whole row green (it was the first three cells) and takes every colour off a placeholder's row (the user's own green marks on placeholders were kept before).
137. **(2026-10-30) Ricochet stars: bounce at their centre, twice as dark after.** A ricocheting bullet bounces when its centre reaches the wall of the screen, not its edge (`Evaluate`, `RicochetsLeft`), so a big star goes half out of the screen first. The other bounces (a boss's bounce ops, pulled bullets) stay at the edge. A star that has bounced is drawn half as bright (`RICOCHET_SHADE` 0.5, `HasRicocheted`), for good; other shapes that ricochet are not darkened.
26. **Luna Child: Hidden on the circles and sliders.** In her attacks the objects that start then are hidden like with the Hidden mod (imitated in `DanmakusuPlayfield.UpdateAfterChildren`): a circle or slider head fades out from 40 % of its approach over 30 % of it (`LUNA_HIDDEN_*`), with no approach circle; a slider's body fades out over the whole slider.

## Nue (UFOs)

Code: `UI/Stages/UfoAct.cs`, `UI/BulletField.Stage.cs` (`buildStage`, `nextUfoAttack`), `Bullets/BulletPlanner.cs`, `DanmakusuBalance.cs` (`NUE_*`), `Bosses/BossTuning.cs` (`nue_*`).

### 2026-10-18

1. **Pull phase is never cut short.** A section switch or the end of the map no longer ends her attack early: `BulletPlanner` stops clipping Nue's attack ends to the section end. Other bosses are unchanged. In a spinner section the spinner is her single attack and still ends with it.
2. **Only some bullets are pulled.** First every 3rd, then changed to every 2nd bullet of a ring (`NUE_PULL_EVERY = 2`). Chosen by bullet index, so replays and seeks are the same.
3. **Pull force tripled.** `nue_pull` default 0.3125 → 0.9375.
4. **Slower shooting.** Release frequency halved, then shoot duration doubled again: `NUE_SHOOT_RATE` 2 → 1 → 0.5. Bullet speed unchanged.
5. **Shoot phase capped.** It ends a quarter beat before Nue's next attack, also when that attack is in a later section. After her final attack it runs its full length.
6. **Every pulled bullet is shot out.** A bullet that can't reach its UFO before the pull ends isn't pulled (it flies on). The 600-bullets-per-UFO limit was removed.
7. **Pull layout.** The 8 UFOs are spread evenly round the walls (first at the middle of the top wall, then clockwise), aiming at the centre. Replaces the corner pinwheel.
8. **Cones twice as visible.** `NUE_CONE_ALPHA` 0.25 → 0.5.
9. **Cones warn earlier.** First drawn at fixed spots 2 beats before launch; then changed so the cones ride the UFOs from launch. They're dimmer while the UFOs fly to their places (`NUE_CONE_WARN_SHARE = 0.4`) and full brightness once pulling or shooting starts. Shoot cones fade in while the UFOs gather in the middle.
10. **UFOs slide during the pull.** They move round the walls in the same direction the shoot-phase circle turns (one lap per turn of the circle). Pulled bullets aim at where their UFO will be when they arrive.
11. **Turn direction alternates.** Both phases of a pull-and-shoot sequence turn the same way; the next sequence turns the other way. Counted over the whole map per boss slot, so it doesn't reset at a section change.
12. **Pull everything again.** Every bullet in a cone is pulled (`NUE_PULL_EVERY` removed), except bullets the UFOs already shot out — those are stage bullets, which the pull never looks at.
13. **Pull force doubled.** `nue_pull` default 0.9375 → 1.875.
14. **Rotation halved.** `NUE_TURN_SHARE` 0.25 → 0.125: both the slide round the walls and the turning circle in the shoot phase.
15. **Every-second-bullet rule restored.** Rolled back change 12: only every 2nd bullet of a ring is pulled again (`NUE_PULL_EVERY = 2`). Shot-out bullets are still never pulled.

## Toyohime (free fall, pull towards the player)

### 2026-10-18

1. **Pull halved.** `toyohime_turn` default 0.00075 → 0.000375.
2. **Golden magic circle.** The thin ring round the player is now a golden magic circle (thick outer ring at the edge of the pull, a band of rune marks, a thin inner ring, a faint eight-pointed star) that turns slowly (`TOYOHIME_CIRCLE_*`).
3. **Arrows towards the player.** 8 golden chevrons on the circle keep sliding in towards the player, one after another, each over 2 beats (`TOYOHIME_ARROW*`).
4. **Arrows close to the player; pulled bullets golden.** The arrows now slide from 2.2 to 1.1 circle radii from the player (instead of along the circle's edge) and are smaller (0.9 → 0.6). Every bullet she pulls is marked from the moment the pull turns it (per-bullet `GoldMask`, cleared on rewind) and drawn gold (`TINT_YELLOW`, 85%) with bloom 0.8 (`TOYOHIME_GOLD_*`).
5. **Weaker, every second bullet.** Pull halved again: `toyohime_turn` default 0.000375 → 0.0001875 (slider minimum lowered to 0.00005 so the new default fits). Only every second bullet of a ring is pulled (`TOYOHIME_PULL_EVERY` = 2, by bullet index); the others fly on and stay their own colour. Dropping at the top of the screen still applies to every bullet.

## Junko

### 2026-10-18

1. **Recorded bullets are reshot.** Every 16 beats of her section, every bullet that appeared in those beats (anyone's) is counted, and she shoots that many out again as one ring from where she is, at their average speed (weighted per bullet). Her own reshot rings aren't counted, so the rings don't snowball. A partial window at the end of the section doesn't fire. Rings over 64 bullets are split into several (`MAX_RING_BULLETS`).

## Byakuren (mechanic `Scrolls`, no longer a placeholder)

### 2026-10-18

1. **Remembered spots.** She records where every 5th circle/slider of her section starts. 32 beats later a beam fires from each spot straight at the player (aimed when it starts, never turns), half as wide as Marisa's widest beam, constant width, for 8 beats. Shots are at least 2 beats apart (a spot waits for the one before it), so a dense map fires every 2 beats: 0, 2, 4, 6, 8, ... Shots still fire after her section ends. (`BYAKUREN_RECORD_EVERY`, `_DELAY_BEATS`, `_SHOT_GAP_BEATS`, `_BEAM_BEATS`, `_SPOT_BEAM_SIZE`)
2. **Big star on each spot.** While a spot fires, the boss's big star (as under Marisa) sits on it in Byakuren's colours (lightened purple star, peach glow), spinning (`SpotStarAct`).
3. **Her own beams.** Her attack bursts are 5 slider-width beams from a pentagon of points round her (`BYAKUREN_POINT_RADIUS` 40 px). Each starts pointing straight away from the player and is steered round towards the player, half a turn in 2 beats (`BYAKUREN_HOMING_BEATS`), so the beams curl in.
4. **Spinner.** Her spinner gets the same 5 beams, pointing straight out from her and spinning at the spinner beams' speed instead of homing.
5. **Shake.** All of these are turning beams, which already shake the screen 1–2 px while they fire.

### 2026-10-18 (fix)

6. **Her homing beams were never made.** The burst loop skips beam creation for every boss but Marisa (and Reisen); Byakuren is now let through too.
7. **Clones count.** When Doremy or Sagume shares a section with her, their copied circles and sliders are recorded too (every 5th of originals and copies together, in time order), so beams also fire from copies' spots.
8. **Remembered-spot beams shaped like Marisa's, smaller.** They now start narrow and widen the way Marisa's boss beams do (same start width, growth power 3 and peak factor, all scaled), at a quarter of Marisa's size throughout (`BYAKUREN_SPOT_BEAM_SIZE` 0.5 → 0.25; before, they were a constant half of her widest). The big star on each spot is scaled by the same factor.
9. **No shake; attack beams are headless Sekibanki trails.** None of her beams shake the screen any more (`LaserEmitter.NoShake`). Her attack beams are no longer rotating lasers: when an attack begins, five invisible heads start from the pentagon round her, away from the player, and home like Sekibanki's heads (bat turn rate) for 16 beats (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_LIFE_BEATS`), leaving a beam of normal slider width; every slice is there for 4 beats (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_STAY_BEATS`) and then disappears instead of flying away. It hurts like a slider body (`HeadAct.Beams`, the head act now takes a style). Her spinner keeps its spinning laser beams (no shake).
10. **No beam sound.** Her lasers (spinner beams and remembered-spot beams) make no beam sound (`LaserEmitter.Silent`); her trails never had one.
11. **Homing beams live like Koakuma's bats.** Her headless homing beams no longer stop after 16 beats (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_LIFE_BEATS` unused): each goes on until it has been within 2 osu! circle radii of the player for 8 beats in all (`KOAKUMA_BAT_FADE_RADIUS_CIRCLES`, `KOAKUMA_BAT_FADE_BEATS`), or has left the playfield (60 s at most, the bats' limit). Slices still vanish 4 beats after they're laid.
12. **Remembered spots throw homing beams.** The small Master Sparks from the remembered spots are gone: 32 beats after every fifth circle or slider (at least 2 beats after the spot before) five homing beams like hers start from a pentagon round that spot, away from the player (`BossPeriod.SpotThrows`, `HeadAct` style `FromSpots`). The big star on the spot went with the Master Sparks.
13. **The big star is back, briefly, and hurts.** When a remembered spot throws its beams, the big star in her colours flashes up there: it fades in over 250 ms, stays for 1 beat (`BYAKUREN_SPOT_STAR_BEATS`), fades out over 250 ms, spinning. While it is mostly there it hurts like a slider body (its body, at the bullet hitbox share). It covers every Byakuren section now (it used to be registered for her first one only).

## Hong Meiling (`Qi`)

### 2026-10-18

1. **Every second pulled bullet flies on.** Of the bullets she draws in (counted in the order she takes them), every second one is still fed into the ball (it counts for its size, speed and colour) but does not disappear: when it reaches her it flies on through her the same way at its own speed (not the pull speed), harmless while it is drawn in and hurting again from there (`StageBullet.GhostUntil`, fully visible).
2. **Every second bullet is left alone instead.** Replaces 1. Of the bullets that come within her reach (counted in the order they do), every second one is not drawn in at all: it is not fed into the ball and flies on as it was. The others are drawn in and fed into the ball as before.

## Urumi Ushizaki (new mechanic `Momentum`, no longer a placeholder)

### 2026-10-19

1. **Pushes.** No pauses (`pause` 0 like Zanmu). Every 8 beats (`URUMI_CYCLE_BEATS`) from the start of her section a push is announced (`MomentumAct`, `BossOps.PushMarks`). For 4 beats (`URUMI_PREVIEW_BEATS`) every bullet on the screen, and every bullet shot meanwhile, becomes a bullet of the act: light grey (`URUMI_GREY` 0.75), glowing (`URUMI_BLOOM` 0.8), swelling to 2x (`URUMI_SIZE`, hitbox too) over 300 ms, shaking 1-2 px. A faint grey copy (30 % visible, `URUMI_GHOST_VISIBLE`) shows where each will be 4 beats after the push. Then every one of them gets an extra straight velocity of 0.5x the section's bullet speed (`URUMI_PUSH_SPEED`, adjustable in the boss menu as "Push speed") in the direction boss -> player as it was when the announcement began. Pushes add up, also for bullets caught earlier (`PushPlan`: prefix sums, the offset is a closed form of time; `StageBullet.Pushes/PushFrom`). Beams are not touched.
2. **Own bullets.** Ellipses (they fall towards the player like Toyohime's) in rings of 20 (`URUMI_RING_BULLETS`), one ring every 2 beats (`URUMI_RING_EVERY_BEATS`). The spinner's stars are replaced by the same rings of 20 ellipses. The spinner's beams are gone (they are for every boss but Marisa).
3. Benchmark (Chirality [Asymmetry] 3:00): Urumi alone 4.3 ms per frame, with Lily White 4.8, with Sakuya 6.3, with Zanmu 8.4. No crashes.

## Lily White (`Spring`, no longer a placeholder)

### 2026-10-19

1. **Branches and flowers.** Eight branches grow from the four corners and the middles of the four sides, inwards, each as big as a sub-branch of Yuyuko's tree (`LILY_BRANCH_LENGTH` 0.24 and `LILY_BRANCH_WIDTH` 0.028 of the tree's size, with the sub-branches that come out of it: 15 per anchor), drawn as wood and hurting like a slider body (`LilyAct`). In her attacks every bullet that leaves the screen at any side grows a flower on the branches, evenly spread (the farthest from the flowers there of 8 tried places, `LILY_FLOWER_CANDIDATES`; was: on the branch nearest to where it left) as often (`YUYUKO_SOW_SHARE`) and as fast (`YUYUKO_PETAL_GROW_BEATS`) as Yuyuko's. Each flower bursts into its 5 petals (pink petal bullets) 8 beats after it began to grow (`LILY_FLOWER_BEATS`), not all at the end of the attack. In pauses no flower grows; the others keep growing and burst on time. When she goes the branches grow backwards; a flower still there bursts then.
2. **Own bullets.** Rings of 12 flowers (`LILY_RING_BULLETS`), 2x the size of the flowers that fall from Yuyuko's top (`LILY_FLOWER_SIZE`, new `BulletEmitter.FlowerScale`), every 2 beats, every second ring turned half a step. They flash (the glow pulses 16 times, brighter and brighter, `LILY_FLASH_BLOOM`; `BulletEmitter.Flashes`) and after 8 beats burst into 5 petals (`BulletEmitter.RemoveAfter`, plus 5 `DeriveKind.Explode` rings made out of the flower ring, `makeLilyFlower`). The spinner's stars are replaced by the same rings; its beams are gone.
3. Benchmark: Lily White alone 1.65 ms per frame; with Cirno 32 ms (Cirno's 29k bullets; Sakuya + Cirno is about the same). No crashes.

## Shinmyoumaru Sukuna (new mechanic `Inchling`, no longer a placeholder)

### 2026-10-19

1. **Sizes by distance.** In her attacks every bullet and every beam (slider and spinner beams of the map; hitboxes too) is `SUKUNA_MIN_SIZE` 0.5x at her centre, rising linearly to `SUKUNA_MAX_SIZE` 4x at a distance equal to the playfield's width and beyond. The glow (bloom) goes up the same way, 0 at her to `SUKUNA_BLOOM` 0.9 at the full distance. `InchlingAct` only sets `BulletField.sukunaLevel` / `sukunaCentre` / `sukunaShaking`; the draw (ring bullets, stage bullets, beam widths and per-beam bloom) and the hit checks (rings, stage bullets, beams) read `sukunaGrow(p)`.
2. **Warning.** The level goes from 0 to 1 over the warning before each attack (as long as the warning before her first attack), stays 1 during the attack, and goes back to 0 over as long after it ends. Everything (ring bullets and stage bullets) shakes 1-2 px during the warning. Beams do not shake.
3. **Sound.** Kyouko's wall sound (`kyouko_walls`) at the start of every attack.
4. **Sizes changed** (later 2026-10-19): `SUKUNA_MIN_SIZE` 0.5 → 0.25, `SUKUNA_MAX_SIZE` 4 → 3.
5. Her own bullets stay the default stars (nothing was asked); no beams in her spinner (it is a rule for every boss). Benchmark: 1.1 ms per frame alone, 3.0 with Sakuya, 8.6 with Zanmu, 16 with Cirno. No crashes.

## Ichirin Kumoi and Unzan (new mechanic `Cloud`, no longer a placeholder)

### 2026-10-19

1. **Pictures.** The user's Unzan picture: white background removed (flood fill from the border, soft edge) and cut into `Resources/Textures/unzan_head.png`, `unzan_hand_left.png`, `unzan_hand_right.png` (the head and the right hand touch in the picture: cut by eroding the mask and growing the three pieces back; a small flame curl of the right hand's top left is part of the hand). New `StageTexture.UnzanHead` (layer under the bullets) and `UnzanHandLeft/Right` (layer above them).
2. **Head** behind her (at her place, 4.6 circle radii wide, nudged and tilted up to 25 degrees towards the player).
3. **Throws.** In her attacks (`IchirinAct`) every 8 beats (`ICHIRIN_THROW_EVERY_BEATS`) one hand, left and right in turn, is thrown at the player: a quadratic arc that bulges outwards (`ICHIRIN_ARC` 0.35 of the way's length), speeding up (position goes with the time to the power 1.6, `ICHIRIN_ACCEL_POWER`), 2 beats, to the wall behind the player (the line from her through the player, as it was at the throw; Mokou's wall margin). It stays 8 beats, returns in a straight line in 2 beats. It hurts (1.3 circle radii) while it is out. The throw is made once when it starts and thrown away on a rewind (like Mokou's legs).
4. **Impact.** Mokou's landing rings (`MOKOU_RINGS` 20 x `MOKOU_RING_BULLETS` 8, her spiral and flash, no big circle) at the hit point, the Moon's hit sound of Hecatia (`hecatia_moon_hit`), the field shakes 1-2 px for 4 beats (`ICHIRIN_SHAKE`, `ICHIRIN_SHAKE_BEATS`).
5. **Clap.** In a pause, once both hands are home (at the earliest 1.5 beats into the pause), they come together 3 circle radii in front of her (towards the player) in 1 beat and clap: 4 rings (0.5 beat apart) of 21 ellipses whose directions and speeds make the tip of an arrowhead (`RingShape.Triangle`, aimed at the player), the same shake and the same sound; they go home in 1 beat. If it does not fit into the pause there is no clap.
6. **Chains.** A hand that is home is the free end of a chain of 8 nodes from her (the verlet chain of Hecatia's worlds: same damping and lag), 35 % longer than needed, shaken with a little noise every step and pulled home weakly, so it is unsteady; the hand's picture turns with the last link. While a hand is out the chain's end is held on it (the chain follows as an arm of faint pink puffs). The chains are simulated from the section's start in 4 ms steps and thrown away on a rewind.
7. She shoots nothing else: no own rings, the spinner's stars are gone. Benchmark: 0.9 ms per frame alone, 5.5 with Sakuya, 4.9 with Zanmu, 2.2 with Hecatia.

### 2026-10-19 (later)

**Clap rings are an arrowhead's outline.** Instead of the triangle layout: 28 ellipses (was 21) whose speed follows `ICHIRIN_ARROW_BACK` 0.35 + 0.65 x (1 - angle from the aim / 180 degrees) ^ `ICHIRIN_ARROW_SHARPNESS` 2.4, so the ring is a sharp tip towards the player with round arc sides and a round back, and keeps that shape as it grows.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 2)

1. Impact: half the rings (`ICHIRIN_IMPACT_RINGS` 10, was Mokou's 20 of 8).
2. Twice the size, hands included: `ICHIRIN_HEAD_WIDTH` 4.6 → 9.2, `ICHIRIN_HAND_HEIGHT` 4.2 → 8.4, `ICHIRIN_HAND_HIT` 1.3 → 2.6 (the hit too), home place 2.1/1.5 → 4.2/3, clap distance 3 → 6, arm puffs 2x.
3. Half the speed: throw `ICHIRIN_THROW_BEATS` 2 → 4, return `ICHIRIN_RETURN_BEATS` 2 → 4.
4. Heavier: chain noise `ICHIRIN_CHAIN_NOISE` 0.25 → 0.05, pull home 0.012 → 0.02.
5. Warning: before a throw the hand is drawn slowly back (smooth step, `ICHIRIN_WINDUP_BEATS` 1.5, `ICHIRIN_PULLBACK` 4 circle radii) along the line it will fly on (the direction is chosen when the windup starts) and does not hurt meanwhile. To keep a hand's round at 16 beats (windup 1.5 + throw 4 + stay + return 4) the stay at the wall is `ICHIRIN_HOLD_BEATS` 8 → 6.5.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 3)

1. **No chain physics.** The chains (and the arm puffs along them) are gone (`ICHIRIN_CHAIN_*` removed). A hand that is home swings a little, slowly, as a closed form of time: `ICHIRIN_SWING` 0.3 circle radii once per `ICHIRIN_SWING_BEATS` 3 beats, turning `ICHIRIN_SWING_DEGREES` 5.
2. **Warning.** The act starts with the warning (`period.Start`), not the first attack. The head grows from nothing to its size and the hands fade in over as long as the warning (at least 300 ms); when her phase ends it goes the other way over as long. The windup of a throw now ends at the attack time (`Ready` = attack time - `ICHIRIN_WINDUP_BEATS` 1.5; the throw starts at the attack time), so the first hand is already drawn back in the warning; a hand's round is still 16 beats.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 4)

1. **Hands no longer come back just as they are thrown again.** A hand's round was exactly 16 beats, so the next windup began the very beat it got home (and with a tempo change it could begin before). Now a throw waits until its hand has been home for `ICHIRIN_REST_BEATS` 1 (the windup and throw come later if need be), and the rounds are shorter: windup 1.5 + throw + stay + return + rest ≤ 16.
2. **Half the throw speed:** `ICHIRIN_THROW_BEATS` 4 → 8. To keep the round (1.5 + 8 + 2 + 3 + rest): the stay at the wall `ICHIRIN_HOLD_BEATS` 6.5 → 2, the return `ICHIRIN_RETURN_BEATS` 4 → 3.
3. **Head.** Always behind her as seen from the player (its middle `ICHIRIN_HEAD_BEHIND` 0.3 of its width away from the player) and turned all the way to face the player (before: in front, tilted at most 25 degrees).
4. **Less swing:** `ICHIRIN_SWING` 0.3 → 0.1 circle radii, `ICHIRIN_SWING_DEGREES` 5 → 2.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 5)

1. **Hands swapped.** In the clap the left hand went to the right side (the line across the aim pointed the wrong way for a downward aim): now the left hand always meets the right on its own side.
2. **Flicker.** The turn of a hand jumped at every change (swing 2 degrees to the throw's angle, the throw's tangent, the way home - opposite to the way it flew - back to upright), and the swing started and stopped at once. Now every pose is continuous: the turn goes by the short way from one pose to the next (`lerpAngle`), and the swing fades in and out with how much the hand is at rest (`calm`).
3. **Hands near the head.** Before the first attack the hands stay close to the head (`ICHIRIN_NEAR_X` 2.2, `ICHIRIN_NEAR_Y` 1 circle radii) and move out to their places (4.2, 3) over the warning; when her phase is over they go back to the head as the cloud goes (`spreadAt`).
4. **Sharper pictures.** The source is scaled 2x (Lanczos) and sharpened, and only the darkest ink is pushed towards black (below 95 of 255 brightness, power 2.2; a stronger setting made the shading muddy): the textures are 548x513 (head) and 336x631 / 367x686 (hands), were 276x260 and 172x317 / 186x345. The drawn size is the same.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 6)

**A throw cannot be interrupted.** Before, a throw whose landing was after the end of her phase was cancelled (no impact) and everything faded at the end. Now a throw always runs through windup, flight, stay and return, with its impact rings and sound; the cloud stays until the last hand is home (`finish`: the end of her phase or the last `ReturnEnd`, whichever is later), and the fade-out, the hands' return to the head and the hurting last until then. (The clap still only happens inside her phase.)

### 2026-10-19 (later, 7)

1. **Inner arc.** The arc's side was chosen by the sign of the line's x direction, which is only right for a mostly vertical throw. Now the arc bulges to the side of the line that is farther from her (her place vs the arc's middle), whichever way the hand is thrown.
2. **Walls behind her.** The arc's control point and the place the hand is drawn back to are clamped into the playfield (the same margin as the landing), so a hand no longer goes out through, or is drawn back through, the walls behind her.
3. **Mono fill.** The pink areas of the pictures are two tones now (the average light and the average shadow tone, blended by brightness, 85 %); the ink lines are untouched.
4. **Head swing.** The head sways like the hands, even more slowly (1.5x the period) and as little (`ICHIRIN_SWING`, `ICHIRIN_SWING_DEGREES`).

### 2026-10-19 (later, 8)

**Hands never land on the walls behind her.** The landing was the first wall the line from her through the player reached, so when the player was on her side of the screen the hand hit the wall right behind her as soon as it was thrown. Now the two walls nearest to her (the nearest side and the nearest top or bottom) are ignored: the hand lands on one of the two far walls (`landingOf`). If the line to the player does not reach a far wall inside the playfield, the parts of the direction that point at the walls behind her are turned round (the hand is thrown towards the far side of the screen instead), and the windup goes along that line. The earlier clamp of the arc into the playfield stays.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 9)

1. **Hands at rest face the player** (like the head), not always down: windup, return and clap now turn from / to the way to the player instead of upright.
2. **Bigger outer arc:** `ICHIRIN_ARC` 0.35 → 0.7 of the way's length (still inside the playfield).

### 2026-10-19 (later, 10)

**The whole cloud turns towards the player.** The hands' resting places (and the places they stay near the head in the warning) were fixed left and right of her on the screen while the head and the hands' pictures turned to the player, so with the player at the side both hands stood on the same side of the head. Now all places are in the cloud's own frame: `facing` = the way from her to the player; the head is behind her on that line, the hands are to the left and the right of it (and a little in front), and the clap's two sides are the same. While the player is within 2 circle radii of her the last facing is kept, so the cloud does not spin round when he is on top of her.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 11)

1. **Slow clap, on the player's spot.** The hands come together over `ICHIRIN_CLAP_BEATS` 4 beats (was 1) and go home in as many. They set off when both are home (half a beat after, at the earliest half a beat into the pause) for the place where the player is at that moment (kept inside the playfield), and it does not change after; the rings go out of that place, aimed away from her. A clap needs 8 beats and a half in the pause or there is none. The hands hurt while they are about to meet, as they meet and a little after (from 30 % of the approach before to 20 % of the return after).
2. **Wider arc:** `ICHIRIN_ARC` 0.7 → 1.2.
3. **Rounder back of the arrowhead ring:** `ICHIRIN_ARROW_BACK` 0.35 → 0.55, `ICHIRIN_ARROW_SHARPNESS` 2.4 → 2 (the back was close to the middle where the bullets lie close together and look pointed).
4. **Flat cartoon pictures.** The fill is now only the light tone or the shadow tone with a narrow step between them, decided on a blurred copy (no paper speckle or halftone dots); the edge of the ink keeps its own darkness so the outline has no halo.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 12)

**White specks and fringe gone from the Unzan pictures.** (1) The flat fill skipped everything brighter than 226, so white specks inside the shapes (and next to the ink) stayed: now it is applied to every kept pixel, after the background is known, with the blurred brightness capped at 215 so the white of the background does not pull the edge pixels to the light tone. (2) The light fringe outside the outlines (what the JPEG left between the ink and the white): kept pixels brighter than 195 that touch the background become background, five rounds.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 13)

**Unzan pictures redone as a three-colour cartoon.** The picture is no longer touched up pixel by pixel (that left shadow speckles, uneven outlines and thin white lines at the edge). Now every pixel is one of three classes - ink (almost black), shadow, light (the average tones of the picture) - decided on a blurred copy (the background counts as ink there), then a majority filter (radius 3 then 2, ink counted 1.8x so thin ink lines stay) swallows every speck and halftone dot and evens the outlines. The pixels within 3 px of the background are always ink, the background is transparent black, and a slight blur (0.8 px) anti-aliases the edge, so there is no white line at the edge. Sizes 549x515 (head), 338x626 / 367x686 (hands).

### 2026-10-19 (later, 14)

1. **The clap is held.** After the hands have met they stay together `ICHIRIN_CLAP_HOLD_BEATS` 2 beats (`Clap.Release`) before they go home (4 beats). They hurt until the release. A clap now takes 4 + 2 + 4 = 10 beats and must fit into the pause: the hands set off a quarter beat after both are home (was half), so with a 16 beat pause and a hand thrown at the middle of the attack it ends at about beat 31 of 32. A longer hold would not fit and there would be no clap.
2. **Snap at the impact.** A hand's turn jumped when it hit the wall (the flight's last tangent -> the straight line of the throw, and again when it set off home). Now the turn the hand has as it hits (`Toss.HoldTurn`, the last tangent of the arc) is kept while it stays and is where the return turns from.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 15)

**The clap's arc.** The hands now go to the clap along an outer arc (a quadratic curve whose middle is pushed outwards, away from the other hand, by `ICHIRIN_CLAP_ARC` 1.0 of the way's length) instead of a straight line, and speed up much more at the end (position goes with the time to the power `ICHIRIN_CLAP_ACCEL_POWER` 3, the throw has 1.6): slow at first, fast as they meet. They go home along the same arc.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 16)

1. **Clap acceleration halved:** `ICHIRIN_CLAP_ACCEL_POWER` 3 → 1.5, so the speed at the meeting is about half of what it was (the clap's length cannot be doubled: it would not fit into the pause).
2. **Unzan aims at the real cursor.** While the cursor is frozen (the character stays where it froze) the cloud used the character's place: the head and hands facing, the way and the landing of a throw, and the place of the clap. Now they use the real cursor (new `BulletField.cursorPosition` / `aimPosition`, set every frame before anything is simulated). The hands still hurt by the character's own place (the hitbox does not move). Other bosses still aim at the character; only Ichirin was changed.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 17)

**Layers of Unzan.** From the bottom: her magic circle, the hands, the head, then the bullets, then her. Before, the circle (part of her drawable, above the bullets layer) was above the head, and the hands were in the front layer above everything (hit objects and her too). Now the head and the hands are both in the layer under the bullets (`stageBack`, the hands drawn first), and on maps with Ichirin her magic circle and its glow are taken out of her drawable (`BossCharacter.CreateCircleProxy`, drawn in `circleSlot` below that layer). Other bosses are not changed. The hands are now under the bullets and the hit objects too.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 18)

**Layers of Unzan, corrected (replaces 17).** The cloud is only to be directly below her, nothing else. Now the order from the bottom is: the bullets and everything else as before, then her magic circle (drawn from a proxy in `circleSlot`, on maps with Ichirin), the hands, the head (a new layer `stageCloud`, hands drawn first), then her. The head and the hands are no longer in the layer under the bullets (`stageBack`) nor the hands in the front layer: they are above the bullets and below the hit objects (like her).

### 2026-10-19 (later, 19)

1. **Ichirin: one warning.** Like Mokou, the warning before her section (the power-up sound and the stars that fly in to her) is the only one; the warnings before her later attacks are gone (`BulletField` `once`, `BossCharacter` `windows`).
2. **Luna Child: the bullets' own edges are smooth too.** Besides the two soft copies, a third (1.15x, 55 %) and the bullet's own body is see-through (`LUNA_BODY_SEETHROUGH` 0.4 x blur), so its sharp edge is not what the player sees; all of it scales with the distance as before. The cap of bullets that get copies is 4000 (was 6000: four instances per bullet now; above it only the glow).
3. **Luna Child: arcs halved.** `LUNA_ARC_SHARE` 0.6 → 0.3 (an arc is 30 % of its share of the circle: the gaps are twice as wide as the arcs now).

### 2026-10-19 (later, 20)

**Luna Child: only the edge smoothing, no cap.** The big soft halos (1.4x and 2.2x copies) and the cap of 4000 bullets are gone (`LUNA_SOFT_SIZE_1/2`, `LUNA_SOFT_ALPHA_1/2`, `LUNA_SOFT_CAP` removed). What is left, for every bullet whatever their number: the see-through body (`LUNA_BODY_SEETHROUGH`), one soft copy under it (1.15x, 55 %) and the glow, all by the distance from her. Two instances per blurred bullet instead of four.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 21)

**Luna Child: the edges are gone.** At full blur the bullet's own body is 85 % see-through (`LUNA_BODY_SEETHROUGH` 0.4 → 0.85) so its outline cannot be seen, and there are two soft copies under it again (`LUNA_SOFT_SIZE_0` 1.2 at 45 %, `LUNA_SOFT_SIZE_1` 1.55 at 22 %), so what is seen is a soft glow that fades out. Still no cap; three instances per blurred bullet.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 22)

**A third hand.** Every section of Ichirin has its own cloud (`IchirinAct`), and a cloud stays until its last hand is home and then goes away over the length of the warning, so where one section follows another (break, kiai, spinner) the old cloud was still there while the next one's warning had brought a new one in: two heads and up to four hands at once. Now a cloud gives way to the next one of the same boss: from the moment the next one starts it fades out over half a warning and is gone (it neither draws nor hurts), so there is never more than one cloud (`NextStart`, set in `registerStage`). A throw that was still going when the next section starts is cut then (the hand fades with the cloud); its rings that are already out stay.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 23)

**Luna Child: half see-through, still smooth.** The body of a blurred bullet is only half see-through at full blur (`LUNA_BODY_SEETHROUGH` 0.85 → 0.5), and the smoothness is kept by three soft copies under it instead of two (`LUNA_SOFT_SIZES` 1.1 / 1.28 / 1.55 x the bullet, `LUNA_SOFT_ALPHAS` 60 / 40 / 20 %; were 1.2 and 1.55 at 45 and 22 %), so the edge fades in small steps. Four instances per blurred bullet.

### 2026-10-19 (later, 24)

**Luna Child: really soft bullets, no layers, not see-through.** The copies under the bullets and the see-through body are gone (`LUNA_SOFT_SIZES`, `LUNA_SOFT_ALPHAS`, `LUNA_BODY_SEETHROUGH` removed): the bullets are as opaque as everyone's again. Instead the renderer has a second, soft sheet (`BulletRenderer.makeSoft`): every cell's picture shrunk to half in the middle of its cell and blurred (6 % of the cell), so a bullet drawn from it twice as big has its own size and an edge that fades into nothing. `BulletInstance.Soft` (0..1) fades the sharp picture into the soft one; Luna sets it by the distance from her (full from half the way out, `LUNA_SOFT_RAMP` 2), the glow stays. One quad per bullet (two while it is between sharp and soft). Knives, talismans, flowers and trains have no soft picture and stay sharp.
